# Śrī Śiva Svarodaya (श्रीशिवस्वरोदय)
### With Hindi Commentary by Mahāmahopādhyāya Pandit Mihirchandra
**Published by Khemraj Shrikrishnadas at Shri Venkateshwar Steam Press, Bombay, 1899 CE (Vikram Samvat 1955)**

---

## Front Matter & Publisher's Preface

### Title Page Inscription
**Śrī Śiva Svarodaya**  
*A Sacred Dialogue between Lord Śiva and Goddess Pārvatī*  
Featuring the Hindi commentary (*Bhāṣā-nuyāda*) rendered by the eminent scholar **Mahāmahopādhyāya Dharma-śāstrāpragaṇya Śrīyuta Pandit Mihirchandra**.  

This text expounds highly practical yogic disciplines including:
- **Rāja Yoga** (The Royal Path of Meditation)
- **Haṭha Yoga** (The Yogic Physical Science)
- **Prāṇāyāma** (Control of Vital Life-Force)
- **Tattva-Vijñāna** (The Science of Elemental Vibrations in Breath)

*Printed and Published by Khemraj Shrikrishnadas at the Shri Venkateshwar Steam Press, Bombay, 1899 CE (Samvat 1955).*  
*(All Rights Reserved by Shri Venkateshwar Press).*

## Introduction (*Bhūmikā*)
**By Mahāmahopādhyāya Pt. Mihirchandra & Publisher Khemraj Shrikrishnadas**

### Introductory Verse (*Doḥā*)
> **अष्टसिद्धि नवनिधि मिलै, मिलै मोक्ष सुख खानि।**  
> **आत्माहू गति भूमि रति, शिवस्वरोदय नामि॥**  
>
> *“Through the practice and knowledge named Śiva Svarodaya, one attains the Eight Supernatural Siddhis (Animā, Mahimā, etc.), the Nine Treasures of Kubera (Nava-nidhi), and the ultimate, limitless bliss of Liberation (Mokṣa). It grants the soul supreme realization, true momentum, and divine absorption in the ultimate reality.”*

### Publisher's & Translator's Preface
Although there exist numerous spiritual tools, vows, rituals, and practices by which seekers navigate the ocean of worldly existence (*bhava-sāgara*) through their own intellect, strength, knowledge, and devotion, the future outcome of actions often remains shrouded in doubt and uncertainty. Consequently, over time, countless paths, sects, and ritualistic doctrines arise. 

However, that alone is worthy of being accepted as Absolute Truth (*Satya*) which yields direct, observable, and empirical results (*Pratyakṣa-guṇa*).

This sacred treatise, **Śiva Svarodaya**, containing the profound dialogue between Goddess Pārvatī (Umā) and Supreme Lord Śiva (Maheśvara), reveals the direct science of the Elements (*Tattva-Jñāna*). By mastering this science—regulating the breath, inspecting the *nāḍīs*, and practicing *prāṇāyāma*—numerous yogis have perfected their lives and manifested the divine power of the Almighty within themselves.

In ancient times, authentic Sanskrit manuscripts of this priceless science were exceedingly rare and difficult to obtain. Recognizing its immense value for humanity, we painstakingly procured an authentic, pure Sanskrit exemplar and commissioned an accessible, clear commentary in Hindi (*Bhāṣāntara*). 

While physical, mental, and verbal actions are continuously performed by all beings, this science elevates a person from an ordinary human being (*nara-rāja*) to a sovereign god-like state (*deva-rāja*). Anyone desiring unshakeable peace, prosperity, and health in this world must study this science with faith in the Guru and dedication of mind. Such a practitioner will enjoy supreme bodily and spiritual happiness, and ultimately attain the eternal realm of bliss.

### Verse 1
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
महेश्वरं नमस्कृत्य शैलजां गणनायकम् ।  
गुरुं च परमात्मानं भजे संसारतारणम् ॥ १ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*maheśvaraṁ namaskṛtya śailajāṁ gaṇanāyakam |*  
*guruṁ ca paramātmānaṁ bhaje saṁsāratāraṇam || 1 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*maheśvaram* = to Supreme Lord Śiva; *namaskṛtya* = having bowed; *śailajām* = to Goddess Pārvatī (daughter of the mountain); *gaṇanāyakam* = to Lord Gaṇeśa; *gurum* = to the Spiritual Preceptor; *ca* = and; *paramātmānam* = to the Supreme Soul; *bhaje* = I worship/salute; *saṁsāratāraṇam* = the redeemer from the ocean of worldly existence.

**English Translation:**  
Having reverently bowed down to Supreme Lord Śiva, Goddess Pārvatī (Shailajā), Lord Gaṇeśa (Gananāyaka), the Spiritual Master (Guru), and the Supreme Soul (Paramātman)—who delivers all beings across the ocean of worldly suffering—I begin this sacred exposition of *Śiva Svarodaya*.

---

---

---

### Verse 2
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
देव्युवाच —  
देवदेव महादेव कृपां कृत्वा ममोपरि ।  
सर्वसिद्धि करं ज्ञानं कथयस्व मम प्रभो ॥ २ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*devyuvāca —*  
*devadeva mahādeva kṛpāṁ kṛtvā mamopari |*  
*sarvasiddhi karaṁ jñānaṁ kathayasva mama prabho || 2 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*devyuvāca* = Goddess Pārvatī spoke; *devadeva* = O God of gods; *mahādeva* = O Supreme Lord Śiva; *kṛpām kṛtvā* = bestowing grace; *mamopari* = upon me; *sarvasiddhi karam* = bestower of all spiritual perfections and success; *jñānam* = divine knowledge; *kathayasva* = please narrate/reveal; *mama* = to me; *prabho* = O Lord.

**English Translation:**  
Goddess Pārvatī said: O God of gods! O Mahādeva! Out of Your infinite compassion upon me, O Lord, please reveal that supreme knowledge which grants all perfections (*sarva-siddhi*) and spiritual accomplishment.

---

---

---

### Verse 3
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
कथं ब्रह्माण्डमुत्पन्नं कथं वा परिवर्तते ।  
कथं विलीयते देव वद ब्रह्माण्डनिर्णयम् ॥ ३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*kathaṁ brahmāṇḍamutpannaṁ kathaṁ vā parivartate |*  
*kathaṁ vilīyate deva vada brahmāṇḍanirṇayam || 3 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*katham* = how; *brahmāṇdam* = the cosmic universe/egg; *utpannam* = is created/originated; *katham vā* = and how; *parivartate* = is sustained/evolves; *katham* = how; *vilīyate* = dissolves/merges back; *deva* = O Lord; *vada* = explain; *brahmāṇdanirṇayam* = the definitive truth regarding the universe.

**English Translation:**  
Goddess Pārvatī asked: O Divine Lord, how did this entire cosmos (*Brahmāṇḍa*) come into existence? How is it maintained and evolved? And how does it undergo ultimate dissolution? Please expound to me the definitive secret of the cosmic universe.

---

---

---

### Verse 4
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
ईश्वर उवाच —  
तत्त्वाद्ब्रह्माण्डमुत्पन्नं तत्त्वेन परिवर्तते ।  
तत्त्वे विलीयते देवि तत्त्वाद्ब्रह्माण्डनिर्णयः ॥ ४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*īśvara uvāca —*  
*tattvādbrahmāṇdamutpannaṁ tattvena parivartate |*  
*tattve vilīyate devi tattvādbrahmāṇdanirṇayaḥ || 4 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*īśvara uvāca* = Lord Śiva replied; *tattvāt* = from the Five Great Elements (Tattvas); *brahmāṇdam* = the universe; *utpannam* = is created; *tattvena* = by the Tattvas; *parivartate* = is sustained; *tattve* = into the Tattvas; *vilīyate* = it dissolves; *devi* = O Goddess; *tattvāt* = from the Tattvas; *brahmāṇdanirṇayaḥ* = is the definitive conclusion of cosmic creation.

**English Translation:**  
Lord Śiva replied: O Goddess! The entire cosmos originates from the subtle *Tattvas* (the elemental principles of Earth, Water, Fire, Air, and Ether), is sustained and governed by the *Tattvas*, and ultimately merges back into the *Tattvas*. The *Tattvas* are the absolute foundation and explanation of the universe.

---

---

---

### Verse 5
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
देव्युवाच —  
तत्त्वमेव परं तत्त्वं निश्चितं तत्त्ववादिभिः ।  
तत्त्वस्वरूपं किं देव तत्त्वमेव प्रकाशय ॥ ५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*devyuvāca —*  
*tattvameva paraṁ tattvaṁ niścitaṁ tattvavādibhiḥ |*  
*tattvasvarūpaṁ kiṁ deva tattvameva prakāśaya || 5 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*devyuvāca* = Goddess Pārvatī spoke; *tattvam eva* = the Tattva alone; *param tattvam* = the supreme reality; *niścitam* = determined/ascertained; *tattvavādibhiḥ* = by the knowers of truth; *tattvasvarūpam* = the true intrinsic nature of Tattvas; *kim* = what is it; *deva* = O Lord; *tattvam eva* = the Tattva itself; *prakāśaya* = please illuminate/reveal.

**English Translation:**  
Goddess Pārvatī said: The seers of essential truth have established that the *Tattva* is the supreme reality. What then, O Lord, is the intrinsic nature (*Svarūpa*) of these *Tattvas*? Kindly illuminate the truth of the *Tattvas* for me.

---

---

---

### Verse 6
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
ईश्वर उवाच —  
निराकारोऽपि भगवान् सुसूक्ष्मो निरञ्जनः ।  
स्वररूपेण देहेऽस्मिन् स्थितः सर्वत्र भासते ॥ ६ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*īśvara uvāca —*  
*nirākāro'pi bhagavān susūkṣmo nirañjanaḥ |*  
*svararūpeṇa dehe'smin sthitaḥ sarvatra bhāsate || 6 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*nirākāraḥ api* = although formless; *bhagavān* = the Supreme Lord; *susūkṣmaḥ* = exceedingly subtle; *nirañjanaḥ* = taintless/pure; *svararūpeṇa* = in the form of Swara (the flow of breath); *dehe asmin* = within this body; *sthitaḥ* = residing; *sarvatra bhāsate* = shines everywhere.

**English Translation:**  
Lord Śiva replied: Although the Supreme Being is formless (*Nirākāra*), infinitely subtle (*Susūkṣma*), and unblemished (*Nirañjana*), He resides within this physical body in the form of **Swara** (the rhythmic movement of vital breath) and illuminates all existence.

---

---

---

### Verse 7
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरज्ञेयानि शास्त्राणि स्वरज्ञेयाश्च वेदकाः ।  
स्वरज्ञेयं पदं सर्वं न मया भाषितं पुरा ॥ ७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svarajñeyāni śāstrāṇi svarajñeyāśca vedakāḥ |*  
*svarajñeyaṁ padaṁ sarvaṁ na mayā bhāṣitaṁ purā || 7 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svarajñeyāni* = known through Swara; *śāstrāṇi* = scriptures; *vedakāḥ ca* = and Vedic revelations; *svarajñeyam* = to be realized through Swara; *padam sarvam* = all spiritual states/realms; *na mayā bhāṣitam purā* = which I have not revealed hitherto.

**English Translation:**  
All scriptures, Vedic revelations, and spiritual realms are to be understood and mastered through the science of **Swara**. This secret knowledge, which illuminates all states of existence, I have not revealed so explicitly before.

---

---

---

### Verse 8
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरो वेदो स्वरो वेदाः स्वरो देवः स्वरो विधिः ।  
स्वररूपं जगत्सर्वं स्वर आत्मा महेश्वरः ॥ ८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svaro vedo svaro vedāḥ svaro devaḥ svaro vidhiḥ |*  
*svararūpaṁ jagatsarvaṁ svara ātmā maheśvaraḥ || 8 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svaraḥ* = Swara (breath flow); *vedaḥ* = is the Veda; *vedāḥ* = is the sum of knowledge; *devaḥ* = is the divine deity; *vidhiḥ* = is cosmic ordinance/law; *svararūpam* = composed of Swara; *jagat sarvam* = the entire universe; *svaraḥ* = Swara is; *ātmā* = the Soul; *maheśvaraḥ* = Supreme Lord Śiva.

**English Translation:**  
Swara is the Veda; Swara is all divine wisdom; Swara is the deity; Swara is the ultimate cosmic law. The entire universe is composed of Swara, and Swara is the Atman—Supreme Maheśvara Himself.

---

---

---

### Verse 9
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सृष्टिस्थितिविनाशानां कारणं स्वर एव हि ।  
गुह्याद्गुह्यतरं ज्ञानं सर्वसम्पत्प्रदायकम् ॥ ९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*sṛṣṭisthitivināśānāṁ kāraṇaṁ svara eva hi |*  
*guhyādguhyataraṁ jñānaṁ sarvasampatpradāyakam || 9 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*sṛṣṭi-sthiti-vināśānām* = of creation, preservation, and destruction; *kāraṇam* = the primary cause; *svaraḥ eva hi* = Swara alone indeed; *guhyāt guhyataram* = more secret than all secrets; *jñānam* = knowledge; *sarva-sampat-pradāyakam* = bestower of all wealth, prosperity, and spiritual fruits.

**English Translation:**  
Swara alone is the fundamental cause behind creation (*Sṛṣṭi*), preservation (*Sthiti*), and destruction (*Vināśa*). It is the most esoteric secret among all secrets, granting all wealth, perfections, and divine prosperity.

---

---

---

### Verse 10
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सूक्ष्मात्सूक्ष्मतरं ज्ञानं सुबोधं सत्यप्रत्ययम् ।  
अद्भुतं नास्तिके लोके आधारं त्वास्तिके जने ॥ १० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*sūkṣmātsūkṣmataraṁ jñānaṁ subodhaṁ satyapratyayam |*  
*adbhutaṁ nāstike loke ādhāraṁ tvāstike jane || 10 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*sūkṣmāt sūkṣmataram* = subtler than the subtlest; *jñānam* = wisdom; *subodham* = easy to comprehend through practice; *satya-pratyayam* = producing direct, empirical verification of truth; *adbhutam* = miraculous/perplexing; *nāstike loke* = to non-believers/skeptics; *ādhāram* = firm foundation/support; *tu āstike jane* = indeed to faithful believers.

**English Translation:**  
This wisdom is subtler than the subtlest, yet easily verified through direct practice (*Satya-pratyaya*). To skeptics and non-believers, it appears as an astounding mystery; but to faithful seekers (*Āstika*), it serves as an unshakeable foundation of truth.

---

---

---

### Verse 11
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरज्ञानात् परं ज्ञानं न भूतं न भविष्यति ।  
तस्मात् सर्वप्रयत्नेन स्वरज्ञानं विचारयेत् ॥ ११ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svarajñānāt paraṁ jñānaṁ na bhūtaṁ na bhaviṣyati |*  
*tasmāt sarvaprayatnena svarajñānaṁ vicārayet || 11 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svarajñānāt param* = superior to breath knowledge; *jñānam* = knowledge; *na bhūtam* = has never existed in the past; *na bhaviṣyati* = nor will ever exist in the future; *tasmāt* = therefore; *sarva-prayatnena* = with all earnest efforts; *svarajñānam* = the science of Swara; *vicārayet* = one should contemplate and master.

**English Translation:**  
There is no knowledge superior to the science of Swara—neither in the past nor in the future. Therefore, with all diligence and effort, a practitioner should contemplate and master the knowledge of Swara.

---

---

---

### Verse 12
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्रत्यक्षं दृश्यते लोके फलदं सर्वकर्मसु ।  
नास्तिकानां भयङ्करं तु आस्तिकानां सुखावहम् ॥ १२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*pratyakṣaṁ dṛśyate loke phaladaṁ sarvakarmasu |*  
*nāstikānāṁ bhayaṅkaraṁ tu āstikānāṁ sukhāvaham || 12 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*pratyakṣam* = directly perceived; *dṛśyate* = is seen; *loke* = in the world; *phaladam* = yielding fruitful results; *sarvakarmasu* = in all actions/undertakings; *nāstikānām* = to atheists/skeptics; *bhayaṅkaram* = terrifying/perplexing; *tu* = indeed; *āstikānām* = to believers; *sukhāvaham* = bringing peace and happiness.

**English Translation:**  
In this world, Swara knowledge yields direct, empirical results (*Pratyakṣa-phala*) in every undertaking. While it strikes awe and confusion into the hearts of skeptics, it brings supreme happiness and success to devout practitioners.

---

---

---

### Verse 13
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अथ शिष्यलक्षणम् —  
शान्तचित्ते सदाचारे गुरुभक्त्येकमानसे ।  
दृढचित्ते कृतज्ञे च देयं चैव स्वरोदयम् ॥ १३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*atha śiṣyalakṣaṇam —*  
*śāntacitte sadācāre gurubhaktyekamānase |*  
*dṛḍhacitte kṛtajñe ca deyaṁ caiva svarodayam || 13 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*atha śiṣyalakṣaṇam* = now the qualities of a worthy disciple; *śāntacitte* = to one with a tranquil mind; *sadācāre* = of noble and pure conduct; *gurubhakti-ekamānase* = single-mindedly devoted to the Guru; *dṛḍhacitte* = resolute in mind; *kṛtajñe ca* = and grateful for blessings received; *deyam* = should be imparted; *ca eva* = indeed; *svarodayam* = the science of Swara.

**English Translation:**  
**Qualifications of a Worthy Student:**  
This sacred science of Swara should be imparted only to a disciple who possesses a peaceful mind, immaculate conduct (*Sadācāra*), unswerving devotion to the Guru, steadfast determination, and a deeply grateful heart.

---

---

---

### Verse 14
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
दुष्टे च दुर्जने क्रुद्धे नास्तिके गुरुतल्पगे ।  
दीनसत्त्वे दुराचारे स्वरज्ञानं न दीयते ॥ १४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*duṣṭe ca durjane kruddhe nāstike gurutalpage |*  
*dīnasattve durācāre svarajñānaṁ na dīyate || 14 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*duṣṭe* = to the wicked; *ca* = and; *durjane* = malicious persons; *kruddhe* = wrathful/hot-tempered; *nāstike* = atheists/cynics; *gurutalpage* = violators of the Guru's sanctity; *dīnasattve* = weak-willed/cowardly; *durācāre* = corrupt in conduct; *svarajñānam* = breath knowledge; *na dīyate* = must never be given.

**English Translation:**  
**Disqualifications for Instruction:**  
Swara knowledge must **never** be transmitted to those who are wicked, malicious, chronically angry, atheistic, disrespectful towards the Guru, weak-willed, or of corrupt character.

---

---

---

### Verse 15
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
शृणु देवि प्रवक्ष्यामि देहस्थं ज्ञानमुत्तमम् ।  
येन विज्ञातमात्रेण सर्वज्ञत्वं प्रजायते ॥ १५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi dehasthaṁ jñānamuttamam |*  
*yena vijñātamātreṇa sarvajñatvaṁ prajāyate || 15 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*śṛṇu* = listen; *devi* = O Goddess Pārvatī; *pravakṣyāmi* = I shall expound; *dehastham* = residing inside the body; *jñānam uttamam* = supreme wisdom; *yena* = by which; *vijñātamātreṇa* = merely by knowing/realizing; *sarvajñatvam* = omniscience; *prajāyate* = arises/is born.

**English Translation:**  
Listen attentively, O Goddess! I shall now describe that supreme wisdom enshrined within the human body, the mere realization of which bestows omniscience (*Sarvajñatva*).

---

---

---

### Verse 16
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरे वेदार्थशास्त्राणि स्वरे गान्धर्वमुत्तमम् ।  
स्वरे च सर्वत्रैलोक्यं स्वर आत्मस्वरूपकम् ॥ १६ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svare vedārthaśāstrāṇi svare gāndharvamuttamam |*  
*svare ca sarvatrailokyaṁ svara ātmasvarūpakam || 16 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svare* = in Swara; *vedārthaśāstrāṇi* = the purport of Vedas and Shastras; *svare* = in Swara; *gāndharvam uttamam* = the supreme art of music/harmony; *svare ca* = and in Swara; *sarvatrailokyam* = all the three realms (Earth, Atmosphere, Heavens); *svaraḥ* = Swara is; *ātmasvarūpakam* = the essential embodiment of the Atman.

**English Translation:**  
Within Swara lie the deepest meanings of the Vedas, scriptures, and divine music (*Gāndharva-veda*). Within Swara abide all three worlds, and Swara is the direct embodiment of the Soul (*Ātman*).

---

---

---

### Verse 17
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरहीनश्च दैवज्ञो नाथहीनं यथा गृहम् ।  
शास्त्रहीनं यथा वक्त्रं शिरोहीनं च यद्वपुः ॥ १७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svarahīnaśca daivajño nāthahīnaṁ yathā gṛham |*  
*śāstrahīnaṁ yathā vaktraṁ śirohīnaṁ ca yadvapuḥ || 17 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svarahīnaḥ ca* = devoid of Swara knowledge; *daivajñaḥ* = an astrologer/seer; *nāthahīnam* = masterless/ownerless; *yathā gṛham* = just as a house; *śāstrahīnam* = unlettered/scriptureless; *yathā vaktram* = just as a mouth; *śirohīnam* = headless; *ca yadvapuḥ* = just as a torso without a head.

**English Translation:**  
An astrologer or diviner who lacks knowledge of Swara is like a house without a master, a mouth without speech, or a torso without a head—completely lacking true authority and illumination.

---

---

---

### Verse 18
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
नाडीभेदं तथा प्राणतत्त्वभेदं तथैव च ।  
सुषुम्नामिश्रभेदं च यो जानाति स मुक्तिगः ॥ १८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*nāḍībhedaṁ tathā prāṇatattvabhedaṁ tathaiva ca |*  
*suṣumnāmiśrabhedaṁ ca yo jānāti sa muktigaḥ || 18 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*nāḍībhedam* = distinctions of energy channels (Nadis); *tathā* = and; *prāṇa-tattva-bhedam* = variations of Prana and Tattvas; *tathaiva ca* = likewise; *suṣumnā-miśra-bhedam* = the flow of Sushumna and mixed breaths; *yaḥ jānāti* = whoever knows; *saḥ* = that person; *muktigaḥ* = attains liberation (Mukti).

**English Translation:**  
Whoever truly understands the differentiation of the *Nāḍīs*, the subtle dynamics of *Prāṇa* and the *Tattvas*, and the mysterious union of breath in the central *Suṣumnā* channel, attains liberation (*Mukti*).

---

---

---

### Verse 19
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
साकारे वा निराकारे वायुरेव समाश्रयेत् ।  
कथयन्ति शुभाख्यं च स्वरज्ञानं वरानने ॥ १९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*sākāre vā nirākāre vāyureva samāśrayet |*  
*kathayanti śubhākhyaṁ ca svarajñānaṁ varānane || 19 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*sākāre vā* = whether in worldly/form-based matters; *nirākāre* = or in formless/spiritual endeavors; *vāyuḥ eva* = air/breath alone; *samāśrayet* = is the sole foundation; *kathayanti* = wise sages declare; *śubhākhyam ca* = auspicious and illuminating; *svarajñānam* = the knowledge of Swara; *varānane* = O beautiful-faced Goddess.

**English Translation:**  
O beautiful-faced Goddess! Whether engaged in worldly affairs (*Sākāra*) or spiritual, formless contemplation (*Nirākāra*), vital breath (*Vāyu*) is the sole underlying engine. Great sages declare that Swara knowledge is the supreme source of all auspicious accomplishment.

---

---

---

### Verse 20
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
ब्रह्माण्डखण्डपिण्डाद्याः स्वरेणैव निरूपिताः ।  
सृष्टिसंहारकर्ता च स्वरः साक्षन्महेश्वरः ॥ २० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*brahmāṇdakhaṇḍapiṇḍādyāḥ svareṇaiva nirūpitāḥ |*  
*sṛṣṭisaṁhārakartā ca svaraḥ sākṣanmaheśvaraḥ || 20 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*brahmāṇda-khaṇḍa-piṇḍādyāḥ* = cosmic galaxies, elements, individual bodies; *svareṇa eva* = by Swara alone; *nirūpitāḥ* = are fashioned/manifested; *sṛṣṭi-saṁhāra-kartā* = the creator and destroyer of cosmic manifestation; *ca* = and; *svaraḥ* = Swara is; *sākṣāt maheśvaraḥ* = directly Supreme Lord Śiva Himself.

**English Translation:**  
The vast cosmic spheres (*Brahmāṇḍa*) and individual human bodies (*Piṇḍa*) are created and sustained through Swara alone. Swara is the creator and dissolver of existence—directly Supreme Maheśvara Himself.

---

---

---

### Verse 21
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरज्ञानात् परं गुह्यं स्वरज्ञानात् परं धनम् ।  
स्वरज्ञानात् परं ज्ञानं न दृष्टं न च श्रुतम् ॥ २१ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svarajñānāt paraṁ guhyaṁ svarajñānāt paraṁ dhanam |*  
*svarajñānāt paraṁ jñānaṁ na dṛṣṭaṁ na ca śrutam || 21 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svarajñānāt param* = secret higher than Swara wisdom; *guhyam* = secret; *dhanam* = wealth; *jñānam* = knowledge; *na dṛṣṭam* = has never been seen; *na ca śrutam* = nor ever heard of.

**English Translation:**  
There is no secret deeper than Swara knowledge, no wealth greater than Swara knowledge, and no wisdom higher than Swara knowledge—neither seen nor heard of anywhere in creation.

---

---

---

### Verse 22
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
शत्रुंहन्यात् स्वरबलात् तथा मित्रसमागमः ।  
लक्ष्मीप्राप्तिः स्वरबलात् कीर्तिः स्वरबलात् सुखम् ॥ २२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*śatruṁhanyāt svarabalāt tathā mitrasamāgamaḥ |*  
*lakṣmīprāptiḥ svarabalāt kīrtiḥ svarabalāt sukham || 22 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*śatrum hanyāt* = enemies are overcome; *svarabalāt* = by the power of Swara; *tathā* = also; *mitrasamāgamaḥ* = reunion with true friends; *lakṣmīprāptiḥ* = attainment of prosperity/wealth; *kīrtiḥ* = fame/honor; *sukham* = true happiness.

**English Translation:**  
By aligning with the power of Swara, enemies are vanquished, beloved friends are united, wealth and prosperity (*Lakṣmī*) are secured, and unshakeable fame and joy are established.

---

---

---

### Verse 23
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
कन्याप्राप्तिः स्वरबलात् स्वरतो राजदर्शनम् ।  
स्वरेण देवतासिद्धिः स्वरेण क्षितिपो वशः ॥ २३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*kanyāprāptiḥ svarabalāt svarato rājadarśanam |*  
*svareṇa devatāsiddhiḥ svareṇa kṣitipo vaśaḥ || 23 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*kanyāprāptiḥ* = obtaining an auspicious spouse/bride; *svarabalāt* = by Swara power; *svarataḥ* = from Swara; *rājadarśanam* = favorable audience with rulers/monarchs; *svareṇa* = through Swara; *devatāsiddhiḥ* = communion and mastery of deities; *kṣitipaḥ vaśaḥ* = kings and authorities are brought into alignment.

**English Translation:**  
Through the conscious alignment of Swara, one obtains a virtuous spouse, achieves successful audiences with kings and rulers, gains divine communion with deities, and establishes influence over powerful leaders.

---

---

---

### Verse 24
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरेण गम्यते देशो भोज्ये स्वरे तथैव च ।  
लघुदीर्घं स्वरबलात् मञ्चैवेह निवारयेत् ॥ २४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svareṇa gamyate deśo bhojye svare tathaiva ca |*  
*laghudīrghaṁ svarabalāt mañcaiveha nivārayet || 24 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svareṇa* = by Swara; *gamyate deśaḥ* = travel to distant lands is undertaken; *bhojye* = during dining/eating; *tathaiva ca* = likewise; *laghudīrgham* = minor and major bodily excretions (urination and defecation); *mañca eva iha* = physical beds/rest; *nivārayet* = managed/harmonized.

**English Translation:**  
Journeys to foreign lands, the digestion of food, sleeping routines, and even bodily functions (such as urination and defecation) are optimized and kept free of affliction when performed in harmony with Swara.

---

---

---

### Verse 25
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सर्वशास्त्रपुराणादि स्मृतिवेदाङ्गपूर्वकम् ।  
स्वरज्ञानात् परं तत्त्वं नास्ति किञ्चिद्वरानने ॥ २५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*sarvaśāstrapurāṇādi smṛtivedāṅgapūrvakam |*  
*svarajñānāt paraṁ tattvaṁ nāsti kiñcidvarānane || 25 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*sarva-śāstra-purāṇādi* = all Shastras, Puranas, etc.; *smṛti-vedāṅga-pūrvakam* = including Smritis and Vedangas; *svarajñānāt param* = superior to Swara knowledge; *tattvam* = principle/truth; *na asti kiñcit* = there is none whatsoever; *varānane* = O beautiful Goddess.

**English Translation:**  
O beautiful Goddess Pārvatī! Among all the *Śāstras*, *Purāṇas*, *Smṛtis*, and *Vedāṅgas*, there exists no principle or truth superior to the sacred science of **Swara**.

---

---

### Verse 26
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
नामरूपादिकाः सर्वे मिथ्या सर्वेषु विभ्रमाः ।  
अज्ञानमोहिता मूढा यावत्तत्त्वं न विन्दति ॥ २६ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*nāmarūpādikāḥ sarve mithyā sarveṣu vibhramāḥ |*  
*ajñānamohitā mūḍhā yāvattattvaṁ na vindati || 26 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*nāmarūpādikāḥ* = names, forms, and outer designations; *sarve* = all; *mithyā* = illusory/false; *sarveṣu vibhramāḥ* = causes of delusion among all; *ajñānamohitāḥ* = deluded by ignorance; *mūḍhāḥ* = foolish/unawakened persons; *yāvat* = as long as; *tattvam* = the essential truth of breath elements; *na vindati* = is not realized.

**English Translation:**  
As long as the essential truth of the *Tattvas* and breath is not directly realized, all names, forms, and worldly distinctions remain illusory constructs that delude unawakened minds.

---

---

---

### Verse 27
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
शरीरे नाडीव्यूहं च ज्ञातव्यं तत्त्ववेदिभिः ।  
नाड्यस्तु कन्दमध्यात्तु सप्ततिश्च सहस्रकाः ॥ २७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*śarīre nāḍīvyūhaṁ ca jñātavyaṁ tattvavedibhiḥ |*  
*nāḍyastu kandamadhyāttu saptatiśca sahasrakāḥ || 27 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*śarīre* = in the body; *nāḍīvyūham ca* = the network of energy channels (Nadis); *jñātavyam* = must be known; *tattvavedibhiḥ* = by knowers of Tattvas; *nāḍyaḥ tu* = the Nadis indeed; *kandamadhyāt tu* = originating from the center of the root bulb (Kanda); *saptatiḥ ca sahasrakāḥ* = seventy-two thousand (72,000).

**English Translation:**  
Knower of the *Tattvas* must understand the intricate network of subtle energy channels (*Nāḍīs*) within the body. Radiating outward from the central root bulb (*Kanda*, located between the anus and genitals), there are 72,000 *Nāḍīs*.

---

---

---

### Verse 28
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
तासां मध्ये प्रधानास्तु दश नाड्यः प्रतिष्ठिताः ।  
तिर्यगूर्ध्वमधःसंस्थाः प्रवहन्ति निरन्तरम् ॥ २८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*tāsāṁ madhye pradhānāstu daśa nāḍyaḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |*  
*tiryaagūrdhvamadhaḥsaṁsthāḥ pravahanti nirantaram || 28 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*tāsām madhye* = among those 72,000 Nadis; *pradhānāḥ tu* = principal/chief indeed; *daśa nāḍyaḥ* = ten Nadis; *pratiṣṭhitāḥ* = established; *tiryag-ūrdhvam-adhaḥ-saṁsthāḥ* = extending horizontally, upward, and downward; *pravahanti nirantaram* = flowing continuously without interruption.

**English Translation:**  
Among these 72,000 channels, **ten principal Nāḍīs** are paramount. They extend horizontally, upward, and downward throughout the body, conducting vital life energy continuously.

---

---

---

### Verse 29
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
इडा च पिङ्गला चैव सुषुम्ना च तृतीयाका ।  
गान्धारी हस्तिजिह्वा च पूषा चैव यशस्विनी ॥ २९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva suṣumnā ca tṛtīyākā |*  
*gāndhārī hastijihvā ca pūṣā caiva yaśasvinī || 29 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*iḍā ca* = Ida; *piṅgalā ca eva* = and Pingala; *suṣumnā ca tṛtīyākā* = and Sushumna as the third; *gāndhārī* = Gandhari; *hastijihvā ca* = and Hastijihva; *pūṣā ca eva* = Pusha; *yaśasvinī* = Yashasvini.

**English Translation:**  
These major channels are: (1) **Iḍā**, (2) **Piṅgalā**, (3) **Suṣumnā** as the third, (4) **Gāndhārī**, (5) **Hastijihvā**, (6) **Pūṣā**, and (7) **Yaśasvinī**.

---

---

---

### Verse 30
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अलम्बुषा कुहूश्चैव शङ्खिनी दशमी स्मृता ।  
एवं दशविधा नाड्यो ग्रथिताः कन्दमध्यतः ॥ ३० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*alambuṣā kuhūścaiva śaṅkhinī daśamī smṛtā |*  
*evaṁ daśavidhā nāḍyo grathitāḥ kandamadhyataḥ || 30 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*alambuṣā* = Alambusha; *kuhūḥ ca eva* = and Kuhu; *śaṅkhinī daśamī smṛtā* = Shankhini known as the tenth; *evam daśavidhā nāḍyaḥ* = thus these ten types of Nadis; *grathitāḥ* = woven/rooted; *kandamadhyataḥ* = from the central Kanda root bulb.

**English Translation:**  
(8) **Alambuṣā**, (9) **Kuhū**, and (10) **Śaṅkhinī** as the tenth. Thus, these ten primary *Nāḍīs* branch out from the central *Kanda* root.

---

---

---

### Verse 31
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
इडा वामे स्थिता भागे पिङ्गला दक्षिणे स्थिता ।  
सुषुम्ना मध्यदेशे तु गान्धारी वामलोचने ॥ ३१ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*iḍā vāme sthitā bhāge piṅgalā dakṣiṇe sthitā |*  
*suṣumnā madhyadeśe tu gāndhārī vāmalocane || 31 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*iḍā* = Ida; *vāme sthitā bhāge* = situated on the left side; *piṅgalā dakṣiṇe sthitā* = Pingala situated on the right side; *suṣumnā madhyadeśe tu* = Sushumna in the central spinal path; *gāndhārī vāmalocane* = Gandhari leading to the left eye.

**English Translation:**  
**Iḍā** is situated on the left side of the spine, **Piṅgalā** on the right side, and **Suṣumnā** runs directly through the central spinal column. **Gāndhārī** extends to the left eye.

---

---

---

### Verse 32
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
दक्षिणे हस्तिजिह्वा च पूषा कर्णे च दक्षिणे ।  
यशस्विनी वामकर्णे वक्त्रे चाप्यलम्बुषा ॥ ३२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*dakṣiṇe hastijihvā ca pūṣā karṇe ca dakṣiṇe |*  
*yaśasvinī vāmakarṇe vaktre cāpyalambuṣā || 32 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*dakṣiṇe hastijihvā ca* = Hastijihva extends to the right eye; *pūṣā karṇe ca dakṣiṇe* = Pusha to the right ear; *yaśasvinī vāmakarṇe* = Yashasvini to the left ear; *vaktre ca api alambuṣā* = and Alambusha extends to the mouth/throat.

**English Translation:**  
**Hastijihvā** extends to the right eye; **Pūṣā** goes to the right ear; **Yaśasvinī** extends to the left ear; and **Alambuṣā** terminates at the mouth and throat.

---

---

---

### Verse 33
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
कुहूश्च लिङ्गदेशे तु लिङ्गमूले च शङ्खिनी ।  
एवं द्वाराणि संश्रित्य तिष्ठन्ति दश नाडिकाः ॥ ३३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*kuhūśca liṅgadeśe tu liṅgamūle ca śaṅkhinī |*  
*evaṁ dvārāṇi saṁśritya tiṣṭhanti daśa nāḍikāḥ || 33 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*kuhūḥ ca* = and Kuhu; *liṅgadeśe tu* = at the reproductive organ; *liṅgamūle ca śaṅkhinī* = and Shankhini at the root of the organ/anus; *evam dvārāṇi saṁśritya* = thus governing the bodily apertures/gates; *tiṣṭhanti daśa nāḍikāḥ* = abide these ten principal Nadis.

**English Translation:**  
**Kuhū** governs the generative organ, and **Śaṅkhinī** terminates at the excretory region (*Mūlādhāra* anus). Thus, these ten primary *Nāḍīs* govern the bodily gates/apertures.

---

---

---

### Verse 34
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
इडापिङ्गलासौषुम्राः प्राणमार्गे समाश्रिताः ।  
सततं प्राणवाहिन्यः सोमसूर्याग्निदेवताः ॥ ३४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*iḍāpiṅgalāsauṣumnāḥ prāṇamārge samāśritāḥ |*  
*satataṁ prāṇavāhinyaḥ somasūryāgnidevatāḥ || 34 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*iḍā-piṅgalā-sauṣumnāḥ* = Ida, Pingala, and Sushumna; *prāṇamārge samāśritāḥ* = anchored in the main path of vital breath; *satatam prāṇavāhinyaḥ* = continuously carrying life-force; *soma-sūryāgni-devatāḥ* = presiding deities being Moon (Soma), Sun (Surya), and Fire (Agni).

**English Translation:**  
Among all channels, **Iḍā**, **Piṅgalā**, and **Suṣumnā** form the supreme pathways of vital Prāṇa. They flow unceasingly, presided over by the cosmic energies of the Moon (*Soma*), Sun (*Sūrya*), and Fire (*Agni*).

---

---

---

### Verse 35
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्राणोऽपानः समानश्च उदानो व्यान एव च ।  
नागः कूर्मोऽथ कृकलो देवदत्तो धनञ्जयः ॥ ३५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*prāṇo'pānaḥ samānaśca udāno vyāna eva ca |*  
*nāgaḥ kūrmo'tha kṛkalo devadatto dhanañjayaḥ || 35 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*prāṇaḥ apānaḥ samānaḥ ca* = Prana, Apana, and Samana; *udānaḥ vyānaḥ eva ca* = Udana and Vyana; *nāgaḥ kūrmaḥ atha kṛkalaḥ* = Naga, Kurma, and Krikala; *devadattaḥ dhanañjayaḥ* = Devadatta and Dhananjaya.

**English Translation:**  
The vital forces within the body are ten: the five Primary Prāṇas—**Prāṇa**, **Apāna**, **Samāna**, **Udāna**, and **Vyāna**—and the five Secondary Prāṇas—**Nāga**, **Kūrma**, **Kṛkala**, **Devadatta**, and **Dhanañjaya**.

---

---

---

### Verse 36
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
हृदि प्राणो गुदेऽपानः समानो नाभिमण्डले ।  
उदानः कण्ठदेशस्थो व्यानः सर्वशरीरगः ॥ ३६ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*hṛdi prāṇo gude'pānaḥ samāno nābhimandalai |*  
*udānaḥ kaṇṭhadeśastho vyānaḥ sarvaśarīragaḥ || 36 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*hṛdi prāṇaḥ* = Prana resides in the heart/chest; *gude apānaḥ* = Apana in the anus/pelvic region; *samānaḥ nābhimandale* = Samana in the navel center; *udānaḥ kaṇṭhadeśasthaḥ* = Udana in the throat region; *vyānaḥ sarvaśarīragaḥ* = Vyana pervades the entire physical frame.

**English Translation:**  
**Prāṇa** resides in the heart region; **Apāna** operates in the pelvic/anal region; **Samāna** acts in the navel region (governing digestion); **Udāna** dwells in the throat region; and **Vyāna** pervades the entire body, regulating circulation.

---

---

---

### Verse 37
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
उद्गारे नाग आख्यातः कूर्म उन्मीलने तथा ।  
कृकलः क्षुतकरो ज्ञेयो देवदत्तो विजृम्भणे ॥ ३७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*udgāre nāga ākhyātaḥ kūrma unmīlane tathā |*  
*kṛkalaḥ kṣutkaro jñeyo devadatto vijṛmphaṇe || 37 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*udgāre nāga ākhyātaḥ* = Naga causes belching/eructation; *kūrmaḥ unmīlane tathā* = Kurma causes blinking/opening of eyelids; *kṛkalaḥ kṣutkaraḥ jñeyaḥ* = Krikala generates sneezing and hunger; *devadattaḥ vijṛmphaṇe* = Devadatta induces yawning.

**English Translation:**  
**Nāga** causes belching and hiccups; **Kūrma** governs the opening and closing of eyelids; **Kṛkala** induces sneezing; and **Devadatta** brings forth yawning.

---

---

---

### Verse 38
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
न जहाति मृतं चापि सर्वव्यापी धनञ्जयः ।  
एते नाडीषु सर्वासु भ्रमन्ते जीवहूपिणः ॥ ३८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*na jahāti mṛtaṁ cāpi sarvavyāpī dhanañjayaḥ |*  
*ete nāḍīṣu sarvāsu bhramante jīvarūpiṇaḥ || 38 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*na jahāti* = does not leave/abandon; *mṛtam ca api* = even a corpse/dead body; *sarvavyāpī* = all-pervading; *dhanañjayaḥ* = Dhananjaya Vayu; *ete* = these ten Pranas; *nāḍīṣu sarvāsu* = throughout all Nadis; *bhramante* = move/circulate; *jīvarūpiṇaḥ* = forming the living soul's embodiment.

**English Translation:**  
**Dhanañjaya** is all-pervading and does not depart even from a deceased body (causing post-mortem swelling). These ten Prāṇas circulate through all the *Nāḍīs*, sustaining the embodied living soul.

---

---

---

### Verse 39
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्रकटप्राणसञ्चारो लक्ष्यते देहमध्यतः ।  
इडापिङ्गलासौषुम्राभिर्नाडीभिस्तिसृभिर्बुधैः ॥ ३९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*prakaṭaprāṇasañcāro lakṣyate dehamadhyataḥ |*  
*iḍāpiṅgalāsauṣumnābhirnāḍībhistisṛbhirbudhaiḥ || 39 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*prakaṭaprāṇasañcāraḥ* = manifest movement of vital breath; *lakṣyate* = is observed/perceived; *dehamadhyataḥ* = within the body; *iḍā-piṅgalā-sauṣumnābhiḥ* = through Ida, Pingala, and Sushumna; *nāḍībhiḥ tisṛbhiḥ* = three primary Nadis; *budhaiḥ* = by wise yogis.

**English Translation:**  
The manifest movement of vital breath within the torso is clearly observed by wise yogis through the three primary channels: **Iḍā**, **Piṅgalā**, and **Suṣumnā**.

---

---

---

### Verse 40
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
इडा वामे च विज्ञेया पिङ्गला दक्षिणे स्मृता ।  
इडा नाडी स्थिता वामा ततो व्यस्ता च पिङ्गला ॥ ४० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*iḍā vāme ca vijñeyā piṅgalā dakṣiṇe smṛtā |*  
*iḍā nāḍī sthitā vāmā tato vyastā ca piṅgalā || 40 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*iḍā vāme ca vijñeyā* = Ida is known on the left nostril; *piṅgalā dakṣiṇe smṛtā* = Pingala on the right nostril; *iḍā nāḍī sthitā vāmā* = Ida Nadi stays in left lunar flow; *tataḥ vyastā ca piṅgalā* = opposite to it stays Pingala in right solar flow.

**English Translation:**  
**Iḍā** operates through the left nostril and represents the lunar energy; **Piṅgalā** operates through the right nostril and represents the solar energy. They balance each other in polar opposition.

---

---

---

### Verse 41
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
इडायां संस्थितश्चन्द्रः पिङ्गलायां च भास्करः ।  
सुषुम्ना शम्भुरूपा च शम्भुर्देहे स्वरूपातः ॥ ४१ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*iḍāyāṁ saṁsthitaścandraḥ piṅgalāyāṁ ca bhāskaraḥ |*  
*suṣumnā śambhurūpā ca śambhurdehe svarūpataḥ || 41 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*iḍāyām saṁsthitaḥ candraḥ* = Moon resides in Ida; *piṅgalāyām ca bhāskaraḥ* = Sun resides in Pingala; *suṣumnā śambhurūpā ca* = Sushumna is embodiment of Lord Shiva (Shambhu); *śambhuḥ dehe svarūpataḥ* = Shiva resides in body in form of breath.

**English Translation:**  
The **Moon** (*Chandra*) resides in Iḍā; the **Sun** (*Bhāskara*) resides in Piṅgalā; and **Suṣumnā** is the direct form of Lord Śiva (*Śambhu*). Thus Lord Śiva Himself abides within the body as Swara.

---

---

---

### Verse 42
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
हकारो निर्गमे प्रोक्तः सकारेण प्रवेशनाम् ।  
हकारः शिवरूपेण सकारः शक्तिुरुच्यते ॥ ४२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*hakāro nirgame proktaḥ sakāreṇa praveśanam |*  
*hakāraḥ śivarūpeṇa sakāraḥ śaktirucyate || 42 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*hakāraḥ nirgame proktaḥ* = sound 'Ha' is uttered during exhalation; *sakāreṇa praveśanam* = sound 'Sa' during inhalation; *hakāraḥ śivarūpeṇa* = 'Ha' represents Shiva; *sakāraḥ śaktiḥ ucyate* = 'Sa' represents Shakti.

**English Translation:**  
Exhalation naturally produces the sound **'Ha'** (representing Lord Śiva); inhalation draws in the sound **'Sa'** (representing Goddess Śakti). Thus, every breath continuously recites the divine *Haṁsa* / *So'ham* mantra.

---

---

---

### Verse 43
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
शक्तिरूपः स्थितश्चन्द्रो वामनाडीप्रवाहकः ।  
दक्षनाडीप्रवाहश्च शम्भुरूपो दिवाकरः ॥ ४३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*śaktirūpaḥ sthitaścandro vāmanāḍīpravāhakaḥ |*  
*dakṣanāḍīpravāhaśca śambhurūpo divākaraḥ || 43 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*śaktirūpaḥ sthitaḥ candraḥ* = Moon of Shakti nature flows in left Nadi; *vāmanāḍīpravāhakaḥ* = flowing left nostril; *dakṣanāḍīpravāhaḥ ca* = right Nadi flow; *śambhurūpaḥ divākaraḥ* = Sun of Shiva nature.

**English Translation:**  
The lunar flow in the left nostril embodies **Śakti** (cooling, nourishing energy); the solar flow in the right nostril embodies **Śambhu-Divākara** (heating, transforming energy).

---

---

---

### Verse 44
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
श्वासे सकारसंस्थे तु यद्दानं दीयते बुधैः ।  
तद्दानं जीवललोकेऽस्मिन् कोटिकोटिगुणं भवेत् ॥ ४४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*śvāse sakārasaṁsthe tu yaddānaṁ dīyate budhaiḥ |*  
*taddānaṁ jīvaloke'smin koṭikoṭiguṇaṁ bhavet || 44 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*śvāse sakārasaṁsthe tu* = while breath is in inhalation phase ('Sa'); *yat dānam dīyate* = whatever charity is offered; *budhaiḥ* = by wise persons; *tat dānam* = that gift; *jīvaloke asmin* = in this mortal realm; *koṭikoṭiguṇam bhavet* = becomes magnified millions of times.

**English Translation:**  
Whatever gift or act of charity is performed by a wise practitioner while the breath is flowing inward (*Sakāra* / *Inhalation*), yields millions of times greater spiritual merit in this world.

---

---

---

### Verse 45
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अनेन लक्षयेद्योगी चैकचित्तः समाहितः ।  
सर्वमेव विजानीयान्मार्गे वै चन्द्रसूर्ययोः ॥ ४५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*anena lakṣayedyogī caikacittaḥ samāhitaḥ |*  
*sarvameva vijānīyānmārge vai candrasūryayoḥ || 45 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*anena lakṣayet yogī* = by this method the yogi observes; *aikacittaḥ samāhitaḥ* = with single-pointed, serene mind; *sarvam eva vijānīyāt* = knows everything clearly; *mārge vai candrasūryayoḥ* = along the pathways of Moon and Sun.

**English Translation:**  
A yogi whose mind is single-pointed and tranquil observes the inner movements of breath and comes to know all cosmic secrets along the dual pathways of the Moon and Sun.

---

---

---

### Verse 46
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
ध्यायेत्तत्त्वं स्थिरे जीवे अस्थिरे न कदाचन ।  
इष्टसिद्धिर्भवेत्तस्य महालाभो जयस्तथा ॥ ४६ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*dhyāyettattvaṁ sthire jīve asthire na kadācana |*  
*iṣṭasiddhirbhavettasya mahālābho jayastathā || 46 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*dhyāyet tattvam* = contemplate the active Tattva; *sthire jīve* = when breath/life is steady; *asthire na kadācana* = never when breath is agitated/unsteady; *iṣṭasiddhiḥ bhavet tasya* = fulfillment of desires occurs; *mahālābhaḥ jayaḥ tathā* = immense profit and victory.

**English Translation:**  
One should contemplate the elemental *Tattvas* when breath is calm and steady (*Sthira-jīva*), never when breath is chaotic. This practice yields fulfillment of heart's desires, immense prosperity, and decisive victory.

---

---

---

### Verse 47
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
चन्द्रसूर्यसमभ्यासं ये कुर्वन्ति सदा नराः ।  
अतीतानागतज्ञानं तेषां हस्तगतं भवेत् ॥ ४७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*candrasūryasamabhyāsaṁ ye kurvanti sadā narāḥ |*  
*atītānāgatajñānaṁ teṣāṁ hastagataṁ bhavet || 47 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*candra-sūrya-samabhyāsam* = balanced practice of Lunar and Solar breath; *ye kurvanti sadā narāḥ* = people who practice constantly; *atīta-anāgata-jñānam* = knowledge of past and future; *teṣām hastagatam bhavet* = comes into their palm (becomes directly accessible).

**English Translation:**  
Seekers who constantly observe and balance the Solar and Lunar breath movements gain direct knowledge of the past (*Atīta*) and future (*Anāgata*) as if held in the palm of their hand.

---

---

---

### Verse 48
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
वामे चामृतरूपा स्याञ्जगदाप्यायनं परम् ।  
दक्षिणे चरभागेन जगदुत्पादयेत्तदा ॥ ४८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*vāme cāmṛtarūpā syāñjagadāpyāyanaṁ param |*  
*dakṣiṇe carabāgena jagadutpādayettadā || 48 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*vāme ca amṛtarūpā syāt* = left nostril breath is nectarous in nature; *jagat-āpyāyanam param* = supreme nourishment for the world; *dakṣiṇe carabāgena* = right mobile solar nostril; *jagat utpādayet tadā* = generates creative activity in the world.

**English Translation:**  
The left nostril flow (Iḍā) is nectarous and provides supreme vitality and nourishment to the body and world; the right nostril flow (Piṅgalā) generates dynamic heat, creation, and transformative action.

---

---

---

### Verse 49
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
मध्यमा भवति क्रूरा सर्वकर्मसु दुःसहा ।  
सर्वत्र शुभकार्येषु वामा भवति सिद्धिदा ॥ ४९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*madhyamā bhavati krūrā sarvakarmasu duḥsahā |*  
*sarvatra śubhakāryeṣu vāmā bhavati siddhidā || 49 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*madhyamā bhavati krūrā* = middle breath (Sushumna) is intense/fierce; *sarvakarmasu duḥsahā* = destructive for worldly tasks; *sarvatra śubhakāryeṣu* = in all auspicious tasks; *vāmā bhavati siddhidā* = left lunar breath bestows success.

**English Translation:**  
The middle breath (**Suṣumnā**) is fierce and unsuited for ordinary worldly activities; for all auspicious, peaceful, and constructive undertakings, the **left lunar breath** (Iḍā) grants perfection and success.

---

---

---

### Verse 50
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
निर्गमे तु शुभा वामा प्रवेशे दक्षिणा शुभा ।  
चन्द्रः समेषु विज्ञेयो रविस्तु विषमः सदा ॥ ५० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*nirgame tu śubhā vāmā praveśe dakṣiṇā śubhā |*  
*candraḥ sameṣu vijñeyo ravistu viṣamaḥ sadā || 50 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*nirgame tu śubhā vāmā* = when stepping out/departing, left breath is auspicious; *praveśe dakṣiṇā śubhā* = when entering a house/destination, right breath is auspicious; *candraḥ sameṣu vijñeyaḥ* = Moon corresponds to even days/dates; *raviḥ tu viṣamaḥ sadā* = Sun corresponds to odd days.

**English Translation:**  
When departing from a home or starting a journey, active **left breath** is auspicious; when entering a destination, active **right breath** is auspicious. The Moon governs even dates/tithis, while the Sun governs odd dates.

---

---

---

### Verse 51
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
चन्द्रः स्त्री पुरुषः सूर्यश्चन्द्रो गौरश्च लोहितः ।  
चन्द्रनाडीप्रवाहेण सौम्यकार्याणि कारयेत् ॥ ५१ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*candraḥ strī puruṣaḥ sūryāścandro gauraśca lohitaḥ |*  
*candranāḍīpravāheṇa saumyakāryāṇi kārayet || 51 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*candraḥ strī* = Moon is feminine; *puruṣaḥ sūryaḥ* = Sun is masculine; *candraḥ gauraḥ* = Moon is fair/bright; *lohitaḥ sūryaḥ* = Sun is red/fiery; *candranāḍīpravāheṇa* = during flow of lunar channel; *saumyakāryāṇi kārayet* = one should perform gentle/peaceful activities.

**English Translation:**  
The Moon is feminine, cool, and fair; the Sun is masculine, fiery, and dark-red. While the **lunar breath** flows, one should undertake gentle, peaceful, and long-term constructive tasks.

---

---

---

### Verse 52
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सूर्यनाडीप्रवाहेण रौद्रकर्माणि कारयेत् ।  
सुषुम्णायाः प्रवाहेण मुक्तियुक्तिफलानि च ॥ ५२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*sūryanāḍīpravāheṇa raudrakarmāṇi kārayet |*  
*suṣumṇāyāḥ pravāheṇa muktiyuktiphalāni ca || 52 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*sūryanāḍīpravāheṇa* = during flow of solar right nostril; *raudrakarmāṇi kārayet* = perform fierce/forceful tasks; *suṣumṇāyāḥ pravāheṇa* = during flow of middle Sushumna; *mukti-yukti-phalāni ca* = liberation, yogic union, and spiritual fruits are attained.

**English Translation:**  
While the **solar breath** flows, one should execute severe, courageous, and physical tasks. When the central **Suṣumnā** flows, one should engage exclusively in meditation, prayer, and practices leading to spiritual liberation (*Mukti*).

---

---

---

### Verse 53
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
विषमं समगतं कृत्वा समं च विषमीकृतम् ।  
येन सन्धिः कृतो नित्यं स योगी मुच्यते भवात् ॥ ५३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*viṣamaṁ samagataṁ kṛtvā samaṁ ca viṣamīkṛtam |*  
*yena sandhiḥ kṛto nityaṁ sa yogī mucyate bhavāt || 53 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*viṣamam samagatam kṛtvā* = turning irregular flow into regular; *samam ca viṣamīkṛtam* = controlling the movement of nostrils; *yena sandhiḥ kṛtaḥ nityam* = who perpetually masters the junction (Sandhi); *saḥ yogī* = that yogi; *mucyate bhavāt* = is liberated from mortal samsara.

**English Translation:**  
That yogi who learns to alter breath flow at will—turning active solar breath into lunar or vice-versa—and who perpetually masters the twilight junction (*Sandhi*), gains complete freedom from mortal bondage.

---

---

---

### Verse 54
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अथ इडानाडीकर्तव्यानि कर्माणि —  
स्थिरसौम्यविवाहादि भूषणं वस्त्रभूषणम् ।  
प्रासादपुरवापीनां प्रतिष्ठादीनि कारयेत् ॥ ५४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*atha iḍānāḍīkartavyāni karmāṇi —*  
*sthirasoumyavivāhādi bhūṣaṇaṁ vastrabhūṣaṇam |*  
*prāsādapuravāpīnāṁ pratiṣṭhādīni kārayet || 54 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*atha iḍānāḍīkartavyāni karmāṇi* = tasks to be done during Ida flow; *sthira-soumya-vivāhādi* = permanent, peaceful, marriage rites; *bhūṣaṇam vastrabhūṣaṇam* = wearing new ornaments and fine garments; *prāsāda-pura-vāpīnām* = temples, cities, reservoirs/wells; *pratiṣṭhādīni kārayet* = consecration rites should be done.

**English Translation:**  
**Tasks Ideal for Left Nostril (Iḍā / Lunar Breath):**  
During active left nostril breath, undertake permanent and peaceful activities: marriage ceremonies, purchasing or wearing jewelry and fine clothes, and consecrating temples, homes, wells, and public monuments.

---

---

---

### Verse 55
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
वापीकूपतडागादिदीक्षासन्त्रप्रवेशनम् ।  
औषधं भेषजं चैव स्वामी दर्शनमेव च ॥ ५५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*vāpīkūpataḍāgādidīkṣāsantrapraveśanam |*  
*auṣadhaṁ bheṣajaṁ caiva svāmī darśanameva ca || 55 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*vāpī-kūpa-taḍāgādi* = step-wells, water wells, ponds; *dīkṣā-santra-praveśanam* = spiritual initiation, esoteric mantra study; *auṣadham bheṣajam caiva* = preparing and taking medicine; *svāmī darśanam eva ca* = meeting spiritual masters, elders, or kings.

**English Translation:**  
Digging wells and reservoirs, receiving spiritual mantra initiation (*Dīkṣā*), preparing and administering medical remedies, and seeking audience with spiritual masters or rulers should all be done under left lunar breath.

---

---

---

### Verse 56
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अध्वयानं विषचिकित्सा धर्मकामार्थसाधनम् ।  
शान्तिकं पुष्टिकर्माणि गवां हरणपोषणम् ॥ ५६ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*adhvayānaṁ viṣacikitsā dharmakāmārthasādhanam |*  
*śāntikaṁ puṣṭikarmāṇi gavāṁ haraṇapoṣaṇam || 56 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*adhvayānam* = embarking on long-distance travel; *viṣacikitsā* = treating poison/snakebites; *dharma-kāma-artha-sādhanam* = pursuits of righteousness, noble love, and wealth; *śāntikam puṣṭikarmāṇi* = pacification rites and vitality rituals; *gavām haraṇapoṣaṇam* = acquiring and nourishing cattle/livestock.

**English Translation:**  
Starting long journeys, administering antidotes for poison, engaging in righteous duties (*Dharma*), legitimate wealth generation (*Artha*), peaceful rituals (*Śāntika*), vitality rites (*Puṣṭi*), and livestock care flourish under Iḍā flow.

---

---

---

### Verse 57
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वामिशिक्षा गुरुशिक्षा तत्त्वाध्यानविचारणम् ।  
एतानि सिद्धिकर्माणि वामनाड्यां समाचरेत् ॥ ५७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svāmiśikṣā guruśikṣā tattvādhyānavicāraṇam |*  
*etāni siddhikarmāṇi vāmanāḍyāṁ samācaret || 57 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svāmiśikṣā guruśikṣā* = receiving instructions from superiors and Gurus; *tattvādhyānavicāraṇam* = deep contemplation on Tattvas and Truth; *etāni siddhikarmāṇi* = these success-yielding actions; *vāmanāḍyām samācaret* = should be performed during left nostril breath.

**English Translation:**  
Receiving instruction from teachers, listening to the Guru's wisdom, and engaging in deep elemental meditation yield ultimate success when performed under active left nostril breath.

---

---

---

### Verse 58
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अथ पिङ्गलानाड्यां कर्तव्यानि —  
कठिनं विद्या पठनं स्त्रीसेवापरदारका ।  
नौकायौद्धरणं युद्धं देशविप्लवसेवनम् ॥ ५८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*atha piṅgalānāḍyāṁ kartavyāni —*  
*kaṭhinaṁ vidyā paṭhanaṁ strīsevāparadārakā |*  
*naukāyouddharaṇaṁ yuddhaṁ deśaviplavasevanam || 58 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*atha piṅgalānāḍyām kartavyāni* = tasks for right solar nostril; *kaṭhinam vidyā paṭhanam* = studying difficult sciences/mathematics; *naukā-yoddharaṇam* = launching boats/ships; *yuddham* = warfare/combat; *deśaviplavasevanam* = dealing with national emergencies or rebellions.

**English Translation:**  
**Tasks Ideal for Right Nostril (Piṅgalā / Solar Breath):**  
During active right nostril breath, perform rigorous and intense activities: studying complex, demanding subjects, engaging in physical combat or sports, launching maritime vessels, and managing crises or emergencies.

---

---

---

### Verse 59
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्नानं भोजनमस्याश्च व्यापारव्यायामादिकम् ।  
अस्त्रशस्त्रक्रियादीनि दक्षिणस्य पदे पदे ॥ ५९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*snānaṁ bhojanamasyāśca vyāpāravyāyāmādikam |*  
*astraśastrakriyādīni dakṣiṇasya pade pade || 59 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*snānam bhojanam* = bathing and taking meals; *vyāpāra-vyāyāmādikam* = commercial trade, physical exercise, athletics; *astraśastrakriyādīni* = handling weapons, archery, martial arts; *dakṣiṇasya pade pade* = at every step under right solar breath.

**English Translation:**  
Bathing, eating meals (allowing digestive fire to burn cleanly), intense business negotiations, physical workouts, and weapon training should always be carried out step-by-step under the **right solar breath**.

---

---

---

### Verse 60
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सुषुम्णायाः प्रवाहे तु न किञ्चित् चिन्तयेत् धिया ।  
केवलं परमेशस्य ध्यानमेव समाचरेत् ॥ ६० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*suṣumṇāyāḥ pravāhe tu na kiñcit cintayet dhiyā |*  
*kevalaṁ parameśasya dhyānameva samācaret || 60 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*suṣumṇāyāḥ pravāhe tu* = while middle Sushumna is flowing; *na kiñcit cintayet dhiyā* = think of no worldly matter with intellect; *kevalam parameśasya* = solely of Supreme Lord Shiva; *dhyānam eva samācaret* = practice meditation alone.

**English Translation:**  
When the breath flows through the central **Suṣumnā** channel (both nostrils flowing equally), refrain from engaging in any worldly, transactional, or material thoughts. At that sacred time, dedicate oneself **solely** to deep meditation on the Supreme Lord.

---

---

### Verse 61
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अथ शुक्लकृष्णपक्षस्वरोदयः —  
शुक्लपक्षे तिथिग्राह्यो वामभागे दिवाकरः ।  
कृष्णपक्षे च दक्षे च प्रतिपत्सु निरन्तरम् ॥ ६१ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*atha śuklakṛṣṇapakṣasvarodayaḥ —*  
*śuklapakṣe tithigrāhyo vāmabhāge divākaraḥ |*  
*kṛṣṇapakṣe ca dakṣe ca pratipatsu nirantaram || 61 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*śuklapakṣe* = during the bright fortnight (waxing moon); *tithigrāhyaḥ* = observed according to lunar dates; *vāmabhāge divākaraḥ* = left nostril active at sunrise; *kṛṣṇapakṣe ca dakṣe ca* = and during dark fortnight, right nostril; *pratipatsu nirantaram* = continuously starting on Pratipad (first lunar day).

**English Translation:**  
**Rhythms of Waxing & Waning Fortnights:**  
On the first day (*Pratipad*) of the bright fortnight (**Śukla Pakṣa**), the **left lunar nostril** (Iḍā) must flow at sunrise. On the first day of the dark fortnight (**Kṛṣṇa Pakṣa**), the **right solar nostril** (Piṅgalā) must flow at sunrise.

---

---

---

### Verse 62
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्रतिपद् द्वितीया तृतीया च चन्द्रे वामो भवेत् सदा ।  
चतुर्थी पञ्चमी षष्ठी पिङ्गला दक्षिणे ततः ॥ ६२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*pratipad dvitīyā tṛtīyā ca candre vāmo bhavet sadā |*  
*caturthī pañcamī ṣaṣṭhī piṅgalā dakṣiṇe tataḥ || 62 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*pratipad dvitīyā tṛtīyā ca* = 1st, 2nd, and 3rd tithis; *candre vāmaḥ bhavet sadā* = left lunar flow stays active; *caturthī pañcamī ṣaṣṭhī* = 4th, 5th, and 6th tithis; *piṅgalā dakṣiṇe tataḥ* = right solar Pingala flow thereafter.

**English Translation:**  
In Śukla Pakṣa, during the 1st, 2nd, and 3rd lunar days (*Pratipad, Dvitīyā, Tṛtīyā*), breath flows through the **left nostril** at sunrise. During the 4th, 5th, and 6th days (*Caturthī, Pañcamī, Ṣaṣṭhī*), breath shifts to the **right nostril** at sunrise.

---

---

---

### Verse 63
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सप्तमी नवमी चैव दशमी वामगा स्मृता ।  
एकादशी द्वादशी च त्रयोदशी च पिङ्गला ॥ ६३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*saptamī navamī caiva daśamī vāmagā smṛtā |*  
*ekādaśī dvādaśī ca trayodaśī ca piṅgalā || 63 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*saptamī navamī caiva daśamī* = 7th, 8th/9th, and 10th tithis; *vāmagā smṛtā* = left nostril flow; *ekādaśī dvādaśī ca trayodaśī ca* = 11th, 12th, and 13th tithis; *piṅgalā* = right Pingala flow.

**English Translation:**  
On the 7th, 8th, 9th, and 10th tithis, breath flows through the left nostril at sunrise; on the 11th, 12th, and 13th tithis, it flows through the right nostril at sunrise.

---

---

---

### Verse 64
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
चतुर्दश्यां च राकायां वामगा प्रवहद् इडा ।  
एवं पक्षद्वये ज्ञेयं स्वरचक्रं विचक्षणैः ॥ ६४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*caturdaśyāṁ ca rākāyāṁ vāmagā pravahad iḍā |*  
*evaṁ pakṣadvaye jñeyaṁ svaracakraṁ vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 64 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*caturdaśyām ca rākāyām* = on 14th tithi and Full Moon (Pūrṇimā); *vāmagā pravahat iḍā* = Ida flows through left nostril; *evam pakṣadvaye jñeyam* = thus across both fortnights should be known; *svaracakram vicakṣaṇaiḥ* = the cycle of breath by wise experts.

**English Translation:**  
On the 14th tithi (*Caturdaśī*) and Full Moon (*Pūrṇimā*), the left lunar nostril (Iḍā) flows at sunrise. Thus, wise practitioners calculate the micro-cycle of breath across both fortnights.

---

---

---

### Verse 65
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
घटिकापञ्चकं यावत् एकमेकोऽनिलो वहेत् ।  
अहोरात्रस्य मध्ये तु षट् चक्राणि क्रमेण तु ॥ ६५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*ghaṭikāpañcakaṁ yāvat ekameko'nilo vahet |*  
*ahorātrasya madhye tu ṣaṭ cakrāṇi krameṇa tu || 65 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*ghaṭikāpañcakam yāvat* = for a duration of 5 Ghaṭikās (approx 2 hours / 120 minutes); *ekam ekaḥ anilaḥ vahet* = one single nostril breath flows; *ahorātrasya madhye tu* = within a 24-hour day and night; *ṣaṭ cakrāṇi krameṇa tu* = six complete alternating cycles occur sequentially.

**English Translation:**  
Each nostril flows continuously for a duration of **5 Ghaṭikās** (approximately 2 hours / 120 minutes). Over a full 24-hour day-and-night cycle (60 Ghaṭikās), breath alternates in **12 total shifts** (6 cycles per nostril).

---

---

---

### Verse 66
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
उदये प्रथमं वामो द्वितीये दक्षिणो भवेत् ।  
एवं व्यत्यययोगेन अहोरात्रं निरन्तरम् ॥ ६६ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*udaye prathamaṁ vāmo dvitīye dakṣiṇo bhavet |*  
*evaṁ vyatyayayogena ahorātraṁ nirantaram || 66 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*udaye prathamam vāmaḥ* = at sunrise first left nostril; *dvitīye dakṣiṇaḥ bhavet* = second shift right nostril; *evam vyatyayayogena* = through such alternating succession; *ahorātram nirantaram* = continuously throughout day and night.

**English Translation:**  
If left breath is active at sunrise, the second shift will be right breath, continuing in precise alternating sequence throughout the day and night without interruption.

---

---

---

### Verse 67
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
विषमे विषमो ज्ञेयः समे तु सम एव च ।  
चन्द्रसूर्यस्वरज्ञेन कर्तव्यं कायमात्मनः ॥ ६७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*viṣame viṣamo jñeyaḥ same tu sama eva ca |*  
*candrasūryasvarajñena kartavyaṁ kāyamātmanaḥ || 67 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*viṣame viṣamaḥ jñeyaḥ* = on odd days/dates odd solar breath is known; *same tu sama eva ca* = on even days even lunar breath; *candrasūryasvarajñena* = by knower of Lunar/Solar Swaras; *kartavyam kāyamātmanaḥ* = body duties should be harmonized.

**English Translation:**  
On odd tithis/days, odd solar breath should operate; on even tithis/days, even lunar breath should operate. One who understands this harmony safeguards body, mind, and spirit.

---

---

---

### Verse 68
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
विपरीते यदा चारे ज्ञेयं दुःखं न संशयः ।  
प्रातःकाले विपरीते तु उद्वेगो व्याधिसम्भवः ॥ ६८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*viparīte yadā cāre jñeyaṁ duḥkhaṁ na saṁśayaḥ |*  
*prātaḥkāle viparīte tu udvego vyādhisambhavaḥ || 68 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*viparīte yadā cāre* = when breath flows contrary to rule; *jñeyam duḥkham na saṁśayaḥ* = suffering is to be expected without doubt; *prātaḥkāle viparīte tu* = if contrary at sunrise time; *udvegaḥ vyādhisambhavaḥ* = anxiety, agitation, and onset of illness.

**English Translation:**  
If breath flows contrary to these natural planetary rhythms at sunrise, expect distress and turmoil without doubt. A reversed morning breath portends anxiety, financial loss, or physical illness.

---

---

---

### Verse 69
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सोमबुधगुरुशुक्रेषु वामनाडी प्रशस्यते ।  
रविभौममन्दवारेषु दक्षिणा तु प्रशस्यते ॥ ६९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*somabudhaguruśukreṣu vāmanāḍī praśasyate |*  
*ravibhaumamandavāreṣu dakṣiṇā tu praśasyate || 69 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*soma-budha-guru-śukreṣu* = on Monday, Wednesday, Thursday, and Friday; *vāmanāḍī praśasyate* = left lunar nostril is praised/auspicious; *ravi-bhauma-manda-vāreṣu* = on Sunday, Tuesday, and Saturday; *dakṣiṇā tu praśasyate* = right solar nostril is praised.

**English Translation:**  
**Concordance with Weekdays (Vāra):**  
On **Monday, Wednesday, Thursday, and Friday**, active **left nostril breath** (Iḍā) is auspicious at sunrise. On **Sunday, Tuesday, and Saturday**, active **right nostril breath** (Piṅgalā) is auspicious at sunrise.

---

---

---

### Verse 70
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अथ स्वरपरिवर्तनोपायः —  
शयने विपरीते तु नाडी वामा च दक्षिणा ।  
अङ्गुष्ठेन नासामूलप्रपीडनात् तथा ॥ ७० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*atha svaraparivartanopāyaḥ —*  
*śayane viparīte tu nāḍī vāmā ca dakṣiṇā |*  
*aṅguṣṭhena nāsāmūlaprapīḍanāt tathā || 70 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*atha svaraparivartanopāyaḥ* = now techniques for altering nostril flow; *śayane viparīte tu* = by lying down on opposite side of body; *nāḍī vāmā ca dakṣiṇā* = left or right Nadi switches; *aṅguṣṭhena nāsāmūlaprapīḍanāt tathā* = or by pressing thumb against active nostril root.

**English Translation:**  
**Technique for Switching Nostril Flow:**  
To shift breath from right to left nostril, lie down on your right side; to shift from left to right, lie down on your left side. Alternatively, gently block the active nostril with your thumb or apply armpit pressure.

---

---

---

### Verse 71
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अथ पूर्णसव्यविचारः —  
पूर्णेन कुरुते कार्यं रिक्तेन न कदाचन ।  
सिद्ध्यन्ते सर्वकार्याणि पूर्णनाड्या निरन्तरम् ॥ ७१ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*atha pūrṇasavyavicāraḥ —*  
*pūrṇena kurute kāryaṁ riktena na kadācana |*  
*siddhyante sarvakāryāṇi pūrṇanāḍyā nirantaram || 71 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*pūrṇena kurute kāryam* = perform tasks using the full active nostril side; *riktena na kadācana* = never using empty inactive side; *siddhyante sarvakāryāṇi* = all undertakings succeed; *pūrṇanāḍyā nirantaram* = continuously through the full active Nadi.

**English Translation:**  
**The Principle of Full (*Pūrṇa*) vs. Empty (*Rikta*) Side:**  
Initiate actions using the side of the body where breath is actively flowing (**Pūrṇa** / Full side). Never initiate important actions using the inactive, empty side (**Rikta**). Tasks undertaken through the full side achieve complete fulfillment.

---

---

---

### Verse 72
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
यात्राकाले विवाहे च द्यूते युद्धे नृपेक्षितौ ।  
पूर्णेन पदवििक्षेपः सिद्धिदः सर्वकर्मसु ॥ ७२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*yātrākāle vivāhe ca dyūte yuddhe nṛpekṣitau |*  
*pūrṇena padavikṣepaḥ siddhidaḥ sarvakarmasu || 72 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*yātrākāle vivāhe ca* = during travel time and marriage; *dyūte yuddhe nṛpekṣitau* = in contests/gambling, combat, and meeting kings; *pūrṇena padavikṣepaḥ* = stepping forward first with the leg corresponding to active nostril; *siddhidaḥ sarvakarmasu* = bestows victory in all matters.

**English Translation:**  
When setting out on a journey, entering marriage, participating in contests or combat, or meeting rulers, always **step forward first with the foot corresponding to the active nostril**. This guarantees success in all endeavors.

---

---

---

### Verse 73
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
अथ पूर्णरिक्तहस्तविचारः —  
पूर्णेन हस्तेन गृहीतवस्तु रिक्तेन दत्तं न तु सिद्धिदेति ।  
पूर्णेन तु सर्वमभीष्टकार्यं रिक्तेन शून्यं विद्धि निश्चितं त्वम् ॥ ७३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*pūrṇena hastena gṛhītavastu riktena dattaṁ na tu siddhideti |*  
*pūrṇena tu sarvamabhīṣṭakāryaṁ riktena śūnyaṁ viddhi niścitaṁ tvam || 73 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*pūrṇena hastena gṛhītavastu* = receiving objects with hand of active side; *riktena dattam na tu siddhida* = giving with empty hand brings no fruit; *pūrṇena tu sarvamabhīṣṭakāryam* = all desired goals fulfilled with full side; *riktena śūnyam viddhi* = know that empty side yields emptiness/zero.

**English Translation:**  
Receive items, money, and blessings with the hand corresponding to the **active nostril side**; give items or make offers similarly. Understand with certainty that actions performed with the full side yield fulfillment, while the empty side yields zero.

---

---

---

### Verse 74
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्रश्ने तु पूर्णनाडीस्थः पृच्छकस्य जयं वदेत् ।  
रिक्ते तु निष्फलं ब्रूयात् समासेन विचक्षणः ॥ ७४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*praśne tu pūrṇanāḍīsthaḥ pṛcchakasya jayaṁ vadet |*  
*rikte tu niṣphalaṁ brūyāt samāsena vicakṣaṇaḥ || 74 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*praśne tu* = during a query/divination; *pūrṇanāḍīsthaḥ* = if questioner stands on practitioner's active nostril side; *pṛcchakasya jayam vadet* = declare victory/success for questioner; *rikte tu niṣphalam brūyāt* = if on empty side, declare failure; *samāsena vicakṣaṇaḥ* = concise rule by wise astrologer.

**English Translation:**  
**Predictive Swara Rule for Queries:**  
When an inquirer asks a question, if they stand or sit on the side of the astrologer's **active nostril**, predict victory, health, and gain. If they stand on the **empty nostril side**, predict failure or loss.

---

---

---

### Verse 75
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
दूरादूध्वं यदा याति स्वरः सुस्थो निरन्तरम् ।  
तदा तु मङ्गलाज्ञेया न चेदभ्युदयः क्वचित् ॥ ७५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*dūrādūrdhvaṁ yadā yāti svaraḥ sustho nirantaram |*  
*tadā tu maṅgalājñeyā na cedabhyudayaḥ kvacit || 75 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*dūrāt ūrdhvam yadā yāti* = when breath flows long, steady, and straight; *svaraḥ susthaḥ nirantaram* = breath is well-established continuously; *tadā tu maṅgalā jñeyā* = know that auspicious fortune is present; *na cet abhyudayaḥ kvacit* = otherwise no prosperity occurs.

**English Translation:**  
When the breath flows steady, deep, and unobstructed through a single nostril, know that divine fortune and auspicious outcome are present.

---

---

### Verse 76
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
वामाचारप्रवाहे तु न गच्छेत्पूर्व उत्तरे ।
परिपन्थिभयं तस्य गतोऽसौ न निवर्तते ॥ ७६ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
vāmācārapravāhe tu na gacchetpūrva uttare /
paripanthibhayaṃ tasya gato'sau na nivartate // 76 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
vāmā-ācāra-pravāhe - during the flow of the left Nadi; tu - but; na - not; gacchet - one should go; pūrva - East; uttare - North; paripanthi-bhayaṃ - fear of enemies/highwaymen; tasya - his; gataḥ asau - he who has gone; na - not; nivartate - returns.
**English Translation:**  
One should not travel towards the East or the North during the active flow of the left Nadi (Iḍā). If one goes, he will face the fear of enemies on the way and will not return safely.

---

---

### Verse 77
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
तथा तस्मान्न गन्तव्यं बुधैः सर्वहितैषिभिः ।
तथा तत्र तु याते मृत्युरेव न संशयः ॥ ७७ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
tathā tasmānna gantavyaṃ budhaiḥ sarvahitaiṣibhiḥ /
tathā tatra tu yāte mṛtyureva na saṃśayaḥ // 77 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
tathā - similarly; tasmāt - therefore; na - not; gantavyaṃ - should be travelled; budhaiḥ - by the wise; sarva-hita-eṣibhiḥ - by those desiring the welfare of all; tathā - so; tatra - there; tu - indeed; yāte - on going; mṛtyuḥ eva - death indeed; na saṃśayaḥ - no doubt.
**English Translation:**  
Therefore, the wise who desire the well-being of everyone should not undertake a journey at such a time. If one does go under such conditions, there is no doubt that death will occur.

---

---

### Verse 78
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
शुक्लपक्षद्वितीयायां यदि वहति चन्द्रमाः ।
दृश्यते लाभदः पुंसां सौम्ये सौख्यं प्रजायते ॥ ७८ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
śuklapakṣadvitīyāyāṃ yadi vahati candramāḥ /
dṛśyate lābhadaḥ puṃsāṃ saumye saukhyaṃ prajāyate // 78 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
śukla-pakṣa - bright fortnight; dvitīyāyāṃ - on the second day; yadi - if; vahati - flows; candramāḥ - the moon (Iḍā Nadi); dṛśyate - it is seen; lābhadaḥ - giving profit; puṃsāṃ - to men; saumye - in auspicious tasks; saukhyaṃ - happiness; prajāyate - is born.
**English Translation:**  
If the lunar breath (Iḍā Nadi) flows on the second day of the bright fortnight, it is seen to bring profit to people, and undertaking auspicious tasks yields happiness.

---

---

### Verse 79
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सूर्योदये यदा सूर्यश्चन्द्रश्चन्द्रोदये भवेत् ।
सिध्यन्ति सर्वकार्याणि दिवारात्रिगतान्यपि ॥ ७९ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
sūryodaye yadā sūryaścandrascandrodaye bhavet /
sidhyanti sarvakāryāṇi divārātrigatānyapi // 79 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
sūrya-udaye - at sunrise; yadā - when; sūryaḥ - the sun (Piṅgalā); candraḥ - the moon (Iḍā); candra-udaye - at moonrise; bhavet - is (flowing); sidhyanti - are accomplished; sarva-kāryāṇi - all tasks; divā-rātri-gatāni api - even those occurring during the day or night.
**English Translation:**  
When the solar breath (Piṅgalā Nadi) flows at sunrise, and the lunar breath (Iḍā Nadi) flows at moonrise, all tasks undertaken during the day or night are successfully accomplished.

---

---

### Verse 80
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
चन्द्रकाले यदा सूर्यः सूर्ये चन्द्रोदये भवेत् ।
उद्वेगः कलहो हानिः शुभं सर्वं निवारयेत् ॥ ८० ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
candrakāle yadā sūryaḥ sūrye candrodaye bhavet /
udvegaḥ kalaho hāniḥ śubhaṃ sarvaṃ nivārayet // 80 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
candra-kāle - at the time of the moon; yadā - when; sūryaḥ - the sun flows; sūrye - at the time of the sun; candra-udaye - when the moon rises; bhavet - it is; udvegaḥ - distress; kalahaḥ - quarrel; hāniḥ - loss; śubhaṃ - auspiciousness; sarvaṃ - all; nivārayet - prevents/destroys.
**English Translation:**  
When the solar breath flows during the time meant for the lunar breath, and the lunar breath flows during the time of the solar breath, it causes distress, quarrels, and loss, preventing all auspicious outcomes.

---

---

### Verse 81
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
सूर्यस्य वाहे प्रवदन्ति विज्ञा ज्ञानमगम्यस्य तु निश्चयेन ।
श्वासेन युक्तस्य तु शीतरश्मेः प्रवाहकाले फलमन्यथा स्यात् ॥ ८१ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
sūryasya vāhe pravadanti vijñā jñānamagamyasya tu niścayena /
śvāsena yuktasya tu śītaraśmeḥ pravāhakāle phalamanyathā syāt // 81 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
sūryasya - of the sun (Piṅgalā); vāhe - in the flow; pravadanti - declare; vijñāḥ - the wise men; jñānam - knowledge; agamyasya - of the inaccessible/unknown; tu - indeed; niścayena - with certainty; śvāsena yuktasya - accompanied by the breath; tu - but; śīta-raśmeḥ - of the cool-rayed one (moon); pravāha-kāle - at the time of flow; phalam - the result; anyathā - otherwise; syāt - is.
**English Translation:**  
The wise declare that during the flow of the solar breath (Piṅgalā), knowledge of the unknown or inaccessible can be ascertained with certainty. However, during the flow of the breath of the lunar Nadi (Iḍā), the result is the opposite (such knowledge cannot be obtained).

---

---

### Verse 82
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
यदा प्रत्यूषकाले तु विपरीतोदयो भवेत् ।
चन्द्रस्थाने वहत्यर्को रविस्थाने च चन्द्रमाः ॥ ८२ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
yadā pratyūṣakāle tu viparītodayo bhavet /
candrasthāne vahatyarko ravisthāne ca candramāḥ // 82 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
yadā - when; pratyūṣa-kāle - at dawn; tu - indeed; viparīta-udayaḥ - contrary rise; bhavet - happens; candra-sthāne - in place of the moon; vahati - flows; arkaḥ - the sun; ravi-sthāne - in place of the sun; ca - and; candramāḥ - the moon.
**English Translation:**  
If a contrary flow of breath arises right from the dawn—such that the solar breath flows during the moon's time and the lunar breath flows during the sun's time...

---

---

### Verse 83
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्रथमे मन उद्वेगं धनहानिर्द्वितीयके ।
तृतीये गमनं प्रोक्तमिष्टनाशश्चतुर्थके ॥ ८३ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
prathame mana udvegaṃ dhanahānirdvitīyake /
tṛtīye gamanaṃ proktamiṣṭanāśaścaturthake // 83 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
prathame - on the first (day); mana udvegaṃ - mental distress; dhana-hāniḥ - loss of wealth; dvitīyake - on the second; tṛtīye - on the third; gamanaṃ - travel/departure; proktaṃ - is declared; iṣṭa-nāśaḥ - loss of the desired (thing/person); caturthake - on the fourth.
**English Translation:**  
(Continuing from the previous verse regarding consecutive days of contrary flow:) On the first day it causes mental distress, on the second day loss of wealth, on the third day forced departure or travel is indicated, and on the fourth day, the destruction of what is desired.

---

---

### Verse 84
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
पञ्चमे राजविध्वंसं षष्ठे सर्वार्थनाशनम् ।
सप्तमे व्याधिदुःखानि अष्टमे मृत्युमादिशेत् ॥ ८४ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
pañcame rājavidhvaṃsaṃ ṣaṣṭhe sarvārthanāśanam /
saptame vyādhiduḥkhāni aṣṭame mṛtyumādiśet // 84 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
pañcame - on the fifth; rāja-vidhvaṃsaṃ - ruin of the kingdom/state; ṣaṣṭhe - on the sixth; sarva-artha-nāśanam - destruction of all wealth/aims; saptame - on the seventh; vyādhi-duḥkhāni - diseases and sorrows; aṣṭame - on the eighth; mṛtyum - death; ādiśet - one should predict.
**English Translation:**  
On the fifth day, it causes the ruin of one's domain or state; on the sixth, the destruction of all wealth and aims; on the seventh, it brings diseases and sorrows; and if it continues to the eighth day, death should be predicted.

---

---

### Verse 85
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
त्रिकाले दिनान्यष्टौ विपरीतं यदा वहेत् ।
तदारिष्टफलं प्रोक्तं किञ्चिन्न्यूनं तु शोभनम् ॥ ८५ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
trikāle dinānyaṣṭau viparītaṃ yadā vahet /
tadāriṣṭaphalaṃ proktaṃ kiñcinnyūnaṃ tu śobhanam // 85 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
tri-kāle - in the three times of the day (morning, noon, evening); dināni aṣṭau - for eight days; viparītaṃ - contrary; yadā - when; vahet - it flows; tadā - then; ariṣṭa-phalaṃ - evil/disastrous result; proktaṃ - is declared; kiñcit nyūnaṃ - slightly less (than eight days); tu - but; śobhanam - auspicious/safe.
**English Translation:**  
When the contrary breath flows continuously through all three periods of the day for eight days, disastrous results are declared. If it is slightly less than that duration, it can still be considered somewhat safe.

---

---

### Verse 86
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्रातर्मध्याह्नयोश्चन्द्रः सायाह्ने च दिवाकरः ।
जयो लाभस्तदा प्रोक्तो विपरीते तु वर्जयेत् ॥ ८६ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
prātarmadhyāhnayoścandraḥ sāyāhne ca divākaraḥ /
jayo lābhastadā prokto viparīte tu varjayet // 86 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
prātaḥ-madhyāhnayoḥ - in the morning and afternoon; candraḥ - the moon; sāyāhne - in the evening; ca - and; divā-karaḥ - the sun; jayaḥ - victory; lābhaḥ - profit; tadā - then; proktaḥ - is declared; viparīte - in the opposite case; tu - but; varjayet - one should avoid.
**English Translation:**  
If the lunar breath flows during the morning and midday, and the solar breath flows in the evening, then victory and profit are declared. If the flow is the opposite, one should avoid undertaking tasks.

---

---

### Verse 87
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
वामे वा दक्षिणे वापि यत्र संक्रमते शिवः ।
कृत्वा तत्पादमौदौ च यात्रा भवति सिद्धिदा ॥ ८७ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
vāme vā dakṣiṇe vāpi yatra saṅkramate śivaḥ /
kṛtvā tatpādamādau ca yātrā bhavati siddhidā // 87 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
vāme - in the left; vā - or; dakṣiṇe - in the right; vā api - or even; yatra - where; saṅkramate - flows/enters; śivaḥ - the auspicious breath; kṛtvā - having placed; tat-pādam - that foot; ādau - first; ca - and; yātrā - journey; bhavati - becomes; siddhi-dā - giving success.
**English Translation:**  
Whether the auspicious breath flows in the left or the right nostril, placing the corresponding foot forward first makes the journey successful.

---

---

### Verse 88
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
चन्द्रः समपदः कार्यो रविस्तु विषमः सदा ।
पूर्णपादं पुरस्कृत्य यात्रा भवति सिद्धिदा ॥ ८८ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
candraḥ samapadaḥ kāryo ravistu viṣamaḥ sadā /
pūrṇapādaṃ puraskṛtya yātrā bhavati siddhidā // 88 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
candraḥ - the moon; sama-padaḥ - even-numbered steps (2, 4, 6...); kāryaḥ - should be done; raviḥ - the sun; tu - but; viṣamaḥ - odd-numbered steps (1, 3, 5...); sadā - always; pūrṇa-pādaṃ - the foot corresponding to the active breath; puraskṛtya - placing in front; yātrā - journey; bhavati - becomes; siddhi-dā - successful.
**English Translation:**  
When the lunar breath is active, one should take an even number of steps initially, and when the solar breath is active, an odd number of steps. By putting forward the foot of the active side first, the journey yields success.

---

---

### Verse 89
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
यदङ्गे वहते वायुस्तदङ्गेनैव पाणिना ।
सुप्तोत्थितो मुखं स्पृष्ट्वा लभते वाञ्छितं फलम् ॥ ८९ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
yadaṅge vahate vāyustadaṅgenaiva pāṇinā /
suptotthito mukhaṃ spṛṣṭvā labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam // 89 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
yat-aṅge - in whichever side of the body; vahate - flows; vāyuḥ - the breath; tat-aṅgena - with that side's; eva - only; pāṇinā - by the hand; supta-utthitaḥ - one who has woken up from sleep; mukhaṃ - the face; spṛṣṭvā - having touched; labhate - obtains; vāñchitaṃ - desired; phalam - result.
**English Translation:**  
Upon waking from sleep, if one touches their face with the hand of the side where the breath is flowing, they will obtain their desired fruits.

---

---

### Verse 90
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
परदत्ते तथा ग्राह्ये दाने निर्गमनेऽपि च ।
यदङ्गे वहते नाडी ग्राह्यं तेन कराङ्घ्रिणा ॥ ९० ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
paradatte tathā grāhye dāne nirgamane'pi ca /
yadaṅge vahate nāḍī grāhyaṃ tena karāṅghriṇā // 90 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
para-datte - given by another; tathā - and; grāhye - in taking/receiving; dāne - in giving; nirgamane - in going out; api ca - and also; yat-aṅge - on whichever side; vahate - flows; nāḍī - the current; grāhyaṃ - it should be taken; tena - by that; kara-aṅghriṇā - by the hand or foot.
**English Translation:**  
When receiving something given by another, making a donation, or stepping out of the house, one should use the hand or foot of the side where the active Nadi is flowing.

---

---

### Verse 91
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
न हानिः कलहो नैव कण्टकेनापि विध्यते ।
निवर्तते सुखी चैव सर्वोपद्रववर्जितः ॥ ९१ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
na hāniḥ kalaho naiva kaṇṭakenāpi vidhyate /
nivartate sukhī caiva sarvopadravavarjitaḥ // 91 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
na - not; hāniḥ - loss; kalahaḥ - quarrel; na eva - never; kaṇṭakena - by a thorn; api - even; vidhyate - is pierced; nivartate - he returns; sukhī - happy; ca eva - and indeed; sarva-upadrava-varjitaḥ - free from all troubles.
**English Translation:**  
By doing so, he suffers no loss or quarrel, nor is he even pierced by a thorn. He returns happily, completely free from all troubles.

---

---

### Verse 92
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
गुरुबन्धुनृपामात्येष्वन्येषु शुभदायिनी ।
पूर्णाङ्गे खलु कर्तव्या कार्यसिद्धिर्मनःस्थिता ॥ ९२ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
gurubandhunṛpāmātyeṣvanyeṣu śubhadāyinī /
pūrṇāṅge khalu kartavyā kāryasiddhirmanaḥsthitā // 92 //
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
guru - teacher; bandhu - relative; nṛpa - king; amātyeṣu - ministers; anyeṣu - among others; śubha-dāyinī - giving auspicious results; pūrṇa-aṅge - with the full side (the active side); khalu - indeed; kartavyā - should be done; kārya-siddhiḥ - accomplishment of the task; manaḥ-sthitā - situated in the mind (desired).
**English Translation:**
