Recaka (exhalation) destroys all sins and impurities, enabling the yogi to attain the highest state of yoga. Afterwards, by maintaining a perfectly balanced state, one is able to conquer death itself.

---

---

### Verse 379
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
कुम्भयेत्सहजं वायुं यथाशक्ति प्रकल्पयेत् ।
रेचयेच्चन्द्रमार्गेण सूर्येणापूरयेत्सुधीः ॥ ३७९ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
kumbhayetsahajaṃ vāyuṃ yathāśakti prakalpayet |
recayeccandramārgeṇa sūryeṇāpūrayetsudhīḥ || 379 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
kumbhayet - one should retain; sahajaṃ - natural; vāyuṃ - breath; yathā-śakti - according to capacity; prakalpayet - one should perform; recayet - one should exhale; candra-mārgeṇa - through the lunar path; sūryeṇa - through the sun; āpūrayet - one should inhale; su-dhīḥ - the intelligent one.
**English Translation:**  
The intelligent practitioner should inhale through the solar channel (right nostril), retain the natural breath within according to his capacity, and then exhale through the lunar channel (left nostril).

---

---

### Verse 380
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
चन्द्रं पिबति सूर्यश्च सूर्यं पिबति चन्द्रमाः ।
अन्योन्यकालभावेन जीवेदाचन्द्रतारकम् ॥ ३८0 ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
candraṃ pibati sūryaśca sūryaṃ pibati candramāḥ |
anyonyakālabhāvena jīvedācandratārakam || 380 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
candraṃ - the moon (breath); pibati - drinks; sūryaḥ - the sun; ca - and; sūryaṃ - the sun; pibati - drinks; candramāḥ - the moon; anyonya-kāla-bhāvena - through their mutual alternation of time; jīvet - one lives; ā-candra-tārakam - as long as the moon and stars exist.
**English Translation:**  
When the solar breath absorbs (drinks) the lunar breath, and the lunar breath absorbs the solar breath—through this mutual alternation, the yogi lives as long as the moon and stars exist.

---

---

### Verse 381
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वस्याङ्गे वहते नाडी तन्नाडीरोधनं कुरु ।
मुखबन्धममुञ्चन् वै पवनं जायते युवा ॥ ३८१ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
svasyāṅge vahate nāḍī tannāḍīrodhanaṃ kuru |
mukhabandhamamuñcan vai pavanaṃ jāyate yuvā || 381 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
svasya-aṅge - in one's own body; vahate - flows; nāḍī - the channel (nostril); tat-nāḍī-rodhanaṃ - blocking of that channel; kuru - do; mukha-bandham - the lock of the mouth; amuñcan - not releasing; vai - indeed; pavanaṃ - the breath; jāyate - becomes; yuvā - young.
**English Translation:**  
Whichever Nadi (nostril) is flowing in one's body, one should block that active Nadi. By keeping the mouth tightly closed and not letting the breath escape through it, the yogi regains his youth.

---

---

### Verse 382
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
मुखनासाक्षिकर्णांस्तान् अङ्गुलीभिर्निरोधयेत् ।
तत्त्वोदयमिति ज्ञेयं षण्मुखीकरणं प्रियम् ॥ ३८२ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
mukhanāsākṣikarṇāṃstān aṅgulībhirnirodhayet |
tattvodayamiti jñeyaṃ ṣaṇmukhīkaraṇaṃ priyam || 382 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
mukha - mouth; nāsā - nose; akṣi - eyes; karṇān - ears; tān - them; aṅgulībhiḥ - with the fingers; nirodhayet - one should block; tattva-udayam - the awakening of the Tattvas; iti - thus; jñeyaṃ - is known; ṣaṭ-mukhī-karaṇaṃ - the Shanmukhi Mudra; priyam - pleasing.
**English Translation:**  
One should firmly close the mouth, nose, eyes, and ears with the fingers. This practice is known as the pleasing Shanmukhi Mudra, which directly leads to the awakening and knowledge of the Tattvas (elements).

---

---

### Verse 383
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
तस्य रूपं गतिः स्वादो मण्डलं लक्षणं त्विदम् ।
स वेत्ति मानवो लोके संसर्गादपि मार्गवित् ॥ ३८३ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
tasya rūpaṃ gatiḥ svādo maṇḍalaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tvidam |
sa vetti mānavo loke saṃsargādapi mārgavit || 383 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
tasya - its (of the Tattva); rūpaṃ - form; gatiḥ - movement; svādaḥ - taste; maṇḍalaṃ - shape; lakṣaṇaṃ - characteristics; tu - indeed; idam - this; saḥ - that; vetti - knows; mānavaḥ - man; loke - in the world; saṃsargāt - from association; api - also; mārga-vit - the knower of the path.
**English Translation:**  
Being a knower of the spiritual path, that person accurately understands the form, movement, taste, shape, and unique characteristics of the Tattvas in this world, distinguishing them through their subtle associations.

---

---

### Verse 384
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
निश्चलो निष्कलो योगी न किञ्चिदपि चिन्तयेत् ।
वासनामुन्मनां कृत्वा कालं जयति लीलया ॥ ३८४ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
niścalo niṣkalo yogī na kiñcidapi cintayet |
vāsanāmunmanāṃ kṛtvā kālaṃ jayati līlayā || 384 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
niścalaḥ - motionless; niṣkalaḥ - free from parts/desires; yogī - yogi; na - not; kiñcit - anything; api - even; cintayet - should think; vāsanām - desires; unmanāṃ - beyond the mind; kṛtvā - having made; kālaṃ - time/death; jayati - conquers; līlayā - playfully.
**English Translation:**  
The yogi should remain perfectly motionless and free from all mental fluctuations, thinking of absolutely nothing. By transcending all desires and entering the Unmani state, he playfully and effortlessly conquers death.

---

---

### Verse 385
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
विश्वस्य वेदिका शक्तिर्नेत्राभ्यां परिदृश्यते ।
तत्रस्थं तु मनो यस्य याममात्रं भवेदिह ॥ ३८५ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
viśvasya vedikā śaktirnetrābhyāṃ paridṛśyate |
tatrasthaṃ tu mano yasya yāmamātraṃ bhavediha || 385 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
viśvasya - of the universe; vedikā - knowing; śaktiḥ - power; netrābhyāṃ - through the eyes; paridṛśyate - is seen; tatrasthaṃ - established there; tu - indeed; manaḥ - mind; yasya - whose; yāma-mātraṃ - for just a moment; bhavet - becomes; iha - here.
**English Translation:**  
The supreme power capable of perceiving the entire universe becomes visible through the eyes. One whose mind remains firmly established in that state even for a moment attains supreme success.

---

---

### Verse 386
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
तस्यायुर्वर्धते नित्यं घटिकात्रयमानतः ।
शिवेनोक्तं पुरा तन्त्रे सिद्धसङ्घगणैः श्रुतम् ॥ ३८६ ॥
**IAST Transliteration:**  
tasyāyurvardhate nityaṃ ghaṭikātrayamānataḥ |
śivenoktaṃ purā tantre siddhasaṅghagaṇaiḥ śrutam || 386 ||
**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
tasya - his; āyuḥ - lifespan; vardhate - increases; nityaṃ - daily; ghaṭikā-traya-mānataḥ - by the measure of three ghatikas; śivena - by Shiva; uktaṃ - said; purā - in ancient times; tantre - in the Tantra; siddha-saṅgha-gaṇaiḥ - by the assemblies of Siddhas; śrutam - heard.
**English Translation:**  
His lifespan continuously increases daily by the measure of three ghatikas. This was spoken in ancient times by Lord Shiva in the Tantra texts and was heard amidst the assemblies of perfected beings (Siddhas).

---

---

### Verse 387
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
बद्ध्वा पद्मासनस्था गुदगतपवनं सन्निरुध्याममुच्चैः ।  
स्तस्यापानरन्ध्रं कमनितमनिलप्रणाशक्त्या विशोध्य ॥  
एवं भूतं सुषुम्नाविवरमुपगतं ब्रह्मरन्ध्रे च नीत्वा ।  
निक्षिप्याकाशमार्गे शिवचरणरता यान्ति ते केऽपि धन्याः ॥ ३८७ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*baddhvā padmāsanasthā gudagatapavanaṁ sannirudhyāmamuccaiḥ |*  
*stasyāpānarandhraṁ kamanitamanilapraṇāśaktyā viśodhya ||*  
*evaṁ bhūtaṁ suṣumnāvivaramupagataṁ brahmarandhre ca nītvā |*  
*nikṣipyākāśamārge śivacaraṇaratā yānti te ke'pi dhanyāḥ || 387 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*baddhvā padmāsanasthā* = seated firmly in Lotus Pose (Padmāsana); *gudagata-pavanam sannirudhya* = locking Apana Vayu in lower pelvic center; *apānarandhram... anilaprāṇa-śaktyā viśodhya* = purifying and uniting Apana with Prana force; *suṣumnāvivaram upagatam* = entering the central Sushumna channel; *brahmarandhre ca nītvā* = elevating energy to the crown center (Brahmarandhra); *nikṣipya ākāśamārge* = merging into the supreme void of consciousness; *śivacaraṇaratāḥ yānti* = absorbed in Lord Shiva's lotus feet; *te ke'pi dhanyāḥ* = blessed indeed are those rare yogis!

**English Translation:**  
**The Grand Yogic Ascension to Shiva:**  
Seated firmly in Lotus Pose (*Padmāsana*), drawing the pelvic *Apāna* air upward, uniting it with the vital *Prāṇa* energy in the navel fire, opening the central *Suṣumnā* channel, elevating the radiant Kundalinī energy up to the crown chakra (*Brahmarandhra*), and dissolving into the infinite void of supreme consciousness—blessed indeed are those rare yogis who merge eternally into the lotus feet of Lord Śiva!

---

---

### Verse 388
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
एतन्नाति यो योगी एतत् पठति नित्यशः ।  
सर्वदुःखैर्विनिर्मुक्तो लभते वाञ्छितं फलम् ॥ ३८८ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*etannāti yo yogī etat paṭhati nityaśaḥ |*  
*sarvaduḥkhairvinirmukto labhate vāñchitaṁ phalam || 388 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*etat nāti yo yogī* = the yogi who understands this text; *etat paṭhati nityaśaḥ* = recites/studies this daily; *sarvaduḥkhaiḥ vinirmuktaḥ* = freed from all sorrows and suffering; *labhate vāñchitam phalam* = attains all desired fruits and goals.

**English Translation:**  
That yogi who understands this sacred treatise and recites or studies it daily is completely liberated from all sorrows and attains every desired spiritual and worldly fruit.

---

---

---

### Verse 389
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरज्ञानं नरे यत्र लक्ष्मीः पादतले भवेत् ।  
स्वग्रहे शरीरेऽपि सुखं तस्य सदा भवेत् ॥ ३८९ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svarajñānaṁ nare yatra lakṣmīḥ pādatale bhavet |*  
*svagrahe śarīre'pi sukhaṁ tasya sadā bhavet || 389 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svarajñānam nare yatra* = in whichever person Swara knowledge resides; *lakṣmīḥ pādatale bhavet* = Goddess Lakshmi abides at his feet; *svagrahe śarīre api* = in his home and body; *sukham tasya sadā bhavet* = eternal happiness is ever present for him.

**English Translation:**  
Wherever a knower of Swara resides, Goddess Lakṣmī (prosperity) abides at his feet. Within his home and body, supreme peace and happiness remain forever established.

---

---

---

### Verse 390
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
प्रणवः सर्ववेदानां ब्रह्मणो भास्करो यथा ।  
मर्त्यलोके तथा पूज्यः स्वरज्ञानी पुमानपि ॥ ३९० ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*praṇavaḥ sarvavedānāṁ brahmaṇo bhāskaro yathā |*  
*martyaloke tathā pūjyaḥ svarajñānī pumānapi || 390 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*praṇavaḥ sarvavedānām* = Omkara among all Vedas; *brahmaṇaḥ bhāskaraḥ yathā* = Sun among celestial luminaries; *martyaloke tathā pūjyaḥ* = so is revered in this mortal realm; *svarajñānī pumānapi* = a person who knows the science of Swara.

**English Translation:**  
Just as **Oṁkāra** is supreme among all Vedas, and the **Sun** is supreme among celestial luminaries, so is a **Knower of Swara** revered above all human beings in this mortal world.

---

---

---

### Verse 391
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
नाडीभेदं विजानाति तत्त्वज्ञानं तथैव च ।  
नैव तेन भवेत्तुल्यं लक्षकोटिरसायनम् ॥ ३९१ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*nāḍībhedaṁ vijānāti tattvajñānaṁ tathaiva ca |*  
*naiva tena bhavettulyaṁ lakṣakoṭirasāyanam || 391 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*nāḍībhedam vijānāti* = who understands Nadi channel dynamics; *tattvajñānam tathaiva ca* = and elemental Tattva wisdom; *na eva tena bhavet tulyam* = nothing is equal to it; *lakṣakoṭirasāyanam* = millions of alchemical elixirs.

**English Translation:**  
Understanding the subtle dynamics of the *Nāḍīs* and the wisdom of the *Tattvas* is far superior to millions of alchemical elixirs (*Lakṣa-koṭi-rasāyana*).

---

---

---

### Verse 392
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
एकाक्षरप्रदातारं नाडीभेदविवेचकम् ।  
पृथिव्यां नास्ति तद्द्रव्यं यद्दत्त्वा चानृणो भवेत् ॥ ३९२ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*ekākṣarapradātāraṁ nāḍībhedavivecakam |*  
*pṛthivyāṁ nāsti taddravyaṁ yaddattvā cānṛṇo bhavet || 392 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*ekākṣarapradātāram* = to the Guru who imparts even a single syllable; *nāḍībhedavivecakam* = who reveals the subtle science of Nadis; *pṛthivyām nāsti tat dravyam* = there exists no wealth on Earth; *yat dattvā ca anṛṇaḥ bhavet* = offering which one can ever repay the debt.

**English Translation:**  
To the spiritual Guru who reveals this science of *Nāḍīs* and imparts even a single syllable of this truth, there exists no treasure on Earth that can ever repay the debt owed to him.

---

---

---

### Verse 393
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वरस्तत्त्वे तथा युद्धे देवि वश्यं श्रियस्तथा ।  
गर्भाधाने च रोगेषु क्षणादर्धेन कथ्यते ॥ ३९३ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svarastattve tathā yuddhe devi vaśyaṁ śriyastathā |*  
*garbhādhāne ca rogeṣu kṣaṇādardhena kathyate || 393 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svarastattve tathā yuddhe* = Swara in Tattvas and warfare; *devi vaśyam śriyaḥ tathā* = O Goddess, in harmony, wealth, prosperity; *garbhādhāne ca rogeṣu* = in conception and disease diagnostics; *kṣaṇāt ardhena kathyate* = can be evaluated within half a moment.

**English Translation:**  
O Goddess! The science of Swara illuminates Tattvas, warfare, wealth generation, marital union, conception, and medical diagnostics in half a moment of observation.

---

---

---

### Verse 394
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
एवं प्रवर्तितं लोके प्रसिद्धं सिद्धयोगिभिः ।  
चन्द्रार्कग्रहणे जाप्यं पठतां सिद्धिदायकम् ॥ ३९४ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*evaṁ pravartitaṁ loke prasiddhaṁ siddhayogibhiḥ |*  
*candrārkagrahhaṇe jāpyaṁ paṭhatāṁ siddhidayakam || 394 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*evam pravartitam loke* = thus promulgated in the world; *prasiddham siddhayogibhiḥ* = made famous by Siddha Yogis; *candrārkagrahhaṇe jāpyam* = recited during Solar or Lunar Eclipses; *paṭhatām siddhidayakam* = grants supreme perfections (Siddhis) to reciters.

**English Translation:**  
Thus was this sacred science promulgated in the world and glorified by Siddha Yogis. Reciting or studying this text during **Solar or Lunar Eclipses** bestows ultimate spiritual perfections (*Siddhis*).

---

---

---

### Verse 395
**Devanāgarī Śloka:**  
स्वस्थाने यः स्थितो नित्यं निद्रां भोजनमल्पकम् ।  
चिन्तयेत्परमात्मानं यो वेद स भविष्यति ॥ ३९५ ॥

**IAST Transliteration:**  
*svasthāne yaḥ sthito nityaṁ nidrāṁ bhojanamalpakam |*  
*cintayetparamātmānaṁ yo veda sa bhaviṣyati || 395 ||*

**Word-for-Word Meaning:**  
*svasthāne yaḥ sthitaḥ nityam* = who remains established in one's own seat/soul daily; *nidrām bhojanam alpakam* = eating and sleeping in moderation; *cintayet paramātmānam* = contemplating the Supreme Soul (Paramātman); *yaḥ veda sa bhaviṣyati* = he indeed becomes a true Knower of Swara.

**English Translation:**  
He who remains steadfast in his inner seat (*Svasthāna*), eats and sleeps in moderation, and unceasingly contemplates the Supreme Soul (*Paramātman*)—he indeed becomes a true **Knower of Swara**.

---

### Colophon
**इति श्रीउमामहेश्वरसंवादे स्वरोदयज्ञानं सम्पूर्णम् ॥**  
*iti śrīumāmaheśvarasaṁvāde svarodayajñānaṁ sampūrṇam ||*  

**"Thus ends the Knowledge of Swara in the Sacred Dialogue between Goddess Pārvatī and Supreme Lord Śiva."**
